## SOCIAL POLICY GRI 403-6

The social policy is successfully implemented in the Company that establishes general principles of  regulation  of  basic  guarantees,  compensations  for  employees,  creation  of  comfortable and safe working conditions, expediency and efficiency, attracts and retains highly qualified personnel, increases the competitiveness of the Company, encourages employees to perform high-productivity work that contributes to their well-being, social protection and formation of the corporate culture.

The  Uniform  Collective  Agreement  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  in  force that  protects  the  interests  of  the  parties  to  the  social  partnership is an evidence of high-scale social and labor relations between the Company and personnel and well-established relations between the employer and trade  union.  The  Collective  Bargaining  Agreement  is aligned with the norms of the Labor Code of the Russian Federation, and the Industry Tariff Agreement in the Electric Power Industry of the Russian Federation.

The Company takes measures to improve the health of the employees and their family members, and give sanatorium and resort vouchers for rehabilitation and recreation.

In  2022, the health care resort services were delivered by Russian sanatoria and recreation centers.

Non-state pension provision is a priority task, the solution of which allows the Company to be socially responsible for its employees and non-working  pensioners  registered  with  the  Company,  forming  an additional non-state pension and providing a decent level of welfare after  retirement.  In  2022,  the  Company  financed  the  non-state pension schemes for 4,254 persons (including 2,575 employees of the Company and 1,679 non-working pensioners registered with the Company).

## Employees' insurance

To increase the level of social protection as per the Insurance Coverage Program of the Company, the employees of Rosseti Volga, PJSC are provided with voluntary personal insurance opportunities that include:

<!-- image -->

## VOLUNTARY MEDICAL INSURANCE

The  voluntary  personal  insurance  empowers  employees  to  receive quality  medical  care  at  the  health  care  institutions  specified  in  the insurance  agreement,  and  allows  paying  the  additional  material compensation to the employees (beneficiaries) for permanent disability, health disorders or death as a result of an accident and/ or natural causes.

The Company's insurance protection shall be planned basing on the uniform insurance standards established by the Company's bylaws, and included in  the  Insurance  Coverage  Program  of  Rosseti  Volga, PJSC for the next calendar year.

To select insurance companies for concluding the voluntary personal insurance agreement, the Company conducts open bidding procedures.

The Board of Directors of the Company is responsible for monitoring the  insurance  protection  of  the  Company  in  compliance  with  the Regulation on provision of the insurance coverage for the Company.

## HUMAN RIGHTS

Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  recognizes  the  importance  of  human  capital  assets  for  the  Company's growth, and is committed to offering comfortable and attractive labor conditions that contribute to  fulfilling  potential  of  each  employee  and  to  increasing  engagement  of  the  personnel  in implementation of the corporate objectives.

The Company regularly monitors the human resource indicators, such as analysis of headcount and movement of employees, personnel cost items,  and  labor  efficiency.  This  allows  to  take  effective  personnel decisions and assess management in an effective way.

As a major employer in the regions serviced by the Company, Rosseti Volga, PJSC understands its responsibility for impacting the local labor markets. To recruit the personnel, the Company evaluate professional qualities and competencies of potential employees: their experience, qualification and education background.

## Rosseti Volga, PJSC observes rights of the employees and commits to taking them into account in its operations.

The  employees  are  given  equal  opportunities  for  fulfilling  their potential at workplaces. The results are evaluated in an impartial and fair manner, and the employment and job promotion depends solely on professional abilities, knowledge and skills.

The Company implements the personnel support and development programs, and thus upholds the rights to social support, education, family welfare, artistic freedom, and participation in cultural life.

The Company offers job opportunities for persons with disabilities. The  employment  rate  for  workers  with  disabilities  is  2%  of  the average headcount (excluding workers exposed to harmful and/or hazardous occupational factors).

Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  avoids  any  types  of  discrimination,  forced or  child  labor,  or  engagement  of  persons  under  18  in  jobs  with harmful  and/or  hazardous  working  conditions.  Each  employee is  given  equal  opportunities  for  exercising  their  labor  rights regardless of sex, race, nationality, origin, property, social status, job title, age, place of residence, religion, political opinion, and  other  circumstances  that  are  not  related  to  the  employee's competencies.

Rosseti Volga, PJSC sticks to generally accepted global approaches to  observing  human  rights  and  freedoms,  and  is  committed  to upholding them in its business activities.

The  Company  endeavors  to  maintain  an  open  dialog  with  the employees,  and  allows  every  voice  to  be  heard.  Any  employee is  able  to  contact  the  General  Director  of  the  Company,  and directors of the Company's branches and production departments. The schedule of personal reception and meetings of the General Director  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  is  published  on  the  corporate website  every  year.  In  addition,  all  employees  are  made  aware of the schedules of personal reception and general meetings with directors of the Company's branches and production departments. The employees are able to submit their inquiries using the Online Front  Desk.  Regular  monitoring  of  the  social  situation  at  work allows quickly addressing any challenges that may arise.

In  order  to  ensure  and  maintain  social  stability  in  the  Company, and to develop social partnership, the Company's administration cooperates with the trade unions of the branches (Rosseti Volga, PJSC has seven trade unions). The Company implements common initiatives  for  the  benefit  of  the  employees,  employer,  and  trade unions.

Each employee of Rosseti Volga, PJSC may join the trade union of  the  Company's  branch  on  a  voluntary  basis.    The  employer respects the statutory rights of the trade union.

The Company is strictly guided by the Russian laws, the Universal Declaration of Human Rights, and recognizes the importance and value of fundamental human rights and freedoms declared by the United Nations, including freedom of association, bargaining right, labor rights, right to a favorable environment, and right to health protection.

The Company adopted necessary procedures intended to promptly address any human  right complaints and claims. The key instruments  in  this  area  include  Unified  Contact  Center,  Online Front Desk of Rosseti Volga, and 'Svetlaya Strana' Internet portal. The employees and any other representatives of the stakeholders may submit their inquiries both on the Internet portals or by phone, and directly in the Company's offices (the inquiry shall be made in writing in any local language spoken in the region serviced by the Company). The Company guarantees confidentiality and security of the communications.

## Public announcement of the Company on commitment to respecting and observing human rights

Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  offers  consumers  free  access  to  any  of  three communication channels (face-to-face, remote, and online) depending on their individual capabilities and preferences.

The information on the Company's services and how to access them is published on information boards in publicly accessible places in the Executive Board, branches, PDs, RES, as well as on the Company's website in the section 'Consumers.'

Rosseti Volga, PJSC sticks to the principle of geographical accessibility and comfortable conditions for face-to-face service.

The consumer has a right to be treated by the Company's personnel with respect and attention.

## Procedures and mechanisms of receipt and processing of complaints against human rights violation, mitigation of negative effect on human rights, and human rights legal protection

To provide services remotely, a single toll-free federal number of the call center is available 24/7 across all area serviced by Rosseti Volga, PJSC. The consumer is able to get all necessary information about the Company's core activities by phone.

Online  services  are  available  on  the  corporate  website  of  Rosseti Volga, PJSC in the section 'Consumers.'

This section contains complete information on the services provided by the Company, including technological connection to electric grids, commercial power metering, power transmission services, and valueadded services. The section 'Centralized Customer Service System' contains  full details  on  the  face-to-face  service  infrastructure: addresses and telephones of services centers, open hours, and how to get there from the nearest public transport stops.

The section 'Consumers' accommodates the Online Front Desk and Personal Account.

The Online Front Desk is an online information service used to get consumer feedback without registration and access to the service.

The Personal Account is a combination of targeted online information services  used  for  communicating  with  consumers  on  services of  technological  connection  to  electric  grids,  commercial  power metering,  power  transmission  services,  value-added  services,  and on other issues.

The public Internet portal of electric grid services 'Svetlaya Strana' (the 'Portal') allows consumers to send messages on quality of the electric grid services delivered by Rosseti Volga, PJSC on such topics as 'Systematic power outages', 'Voltage fluctuations/Low voltage', 'Electric  equipment  defects',  and  to  receive  the  result  of  message processing by the specialists of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

All authorized users of the Portal are able to send messages.

The consumer may create an initial message or denial message, if the consumer is not satisfied with a reply to his/her initial message or denial message.

## Information on adopted human rights due diligence policies, and established procedures and mechanisms intended to prevent violations of human rights

The consumers may share their opinion in the form of a thank-you message, complaint or proposal for improving service quality via any communication channels, including client boxes, customer feedback book, and online services.

In addition, the consumers may report low-quality services to Rosseti, PJSC, local bodies of the Russian Federal Service for Surveillance on

Consumer Rights Protection and Human Wellbeing, Rostekhnadzor, tariff  regulation  bodies  of  the  constituent  entities  of  the  Russian Federation,  executive  authorities  of  the  constituent  entities  of  the Russian Federation, and local governments.

The personal data of the consumers are protected in compliance with the laws of the Russian Federation.

To  mitigate  the  risks  of  occupational  injuries  among  the  Company's  staff  and  third  parties, the Company implemented activities under the Comprehensive Program for Mitigating Risks of Injuries among Staff of Rosseti Volga, PJSC and Third Parties at the Company's Power Grid Facilities for 2022 adopted by Order of Rosseti Volga, PJSC No. 1 dated January 10, 2022.

## IN 2022, THE COMPANY IMPLEMENTED THE FOLLOWING INITIATIVES TO PREVENT INJURIES TO THIRD PARTIES:

outreach and awareness-raising activities involving external companies and general public (including through mass media), to inform them of the electrical hazards and the risk of contact with live parts

in  2022,  electrical  safety  checklists  were  made  available  for  distribution  among  children's  upbringing  and  educational institutions and more than 9,000 printed products (bookmarks, stickers) focused on children's electrical safety were produced and distributed among educational institutions over  100  electrical  safety  awareness-raising  meetings  and  briefings  were  conducted  with  members  of  non-governmental fishermen associations

## OCCUPATIONAL SAFETY

In  the  reporting  period,  zero  accidents  and  incidents  occurred  at hazardous production facilities operated by the branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

Pursuant  to  the  Rules  of  Entry  of  Facilities  into  State  Register  of Hazardous Production Facilities adopted by Decree of RF Government No. 1371 dated November 24, 1998, all hazardous production facilities (HPFs) operated by the production departments of the branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were entered into the State Register.

HPFs  (41  facilities)  were  registered  in  compliance  with  the  above Rules, and Registration Certificate No. А51-05556 dated August 25, 2020 was obtained.

## ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION

<!-- image -->

## ECOLOGICAL POLICY GRI 305-1 GRI 102-11

<!-- image -->

One of the key aspects of the Company's responsibility is to maintain a favorable environment and to keep it protected against any negative effects of business operations, to ensure environmental friendliness and sustainability.

In  order  to  ensure  the  successful  operation  of  the  environmental management  system  and  improve  its  efficiency,  to  involve  the Company's entire staff and unite their efforts aimed at continuously improving and achieving environmental goals, Rosseti Volga, PJSC implements  the uniform Environmental Policy of Electric Grid Complex  which  was  adopted  by  the  Company  by  resolution  of  the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC dated September 15, 2020

(Minutes No. 12 dated September 15, 2020). The Environmental Policy of Electric Grid Complex defined the uniform approach that shall be used  by  Rosseti  Group  of  Companies  to  fulfill  the  environmental tasks  set  for  the  power  grid  complex,  and  to  gradually  limit  the environmental impact of the fuel and energy complex by decreasing pollutant  emissions  and  reducing  the  amount  of  production  and consumption wastes.

Aiming at improving the efficiency of the environmental protection management system and at achieving the target indicators of the Environmental Policy in 2022, Rosseti Volga, PJSC implemented the planned environmental initiatives in the following areas:

-  protection of atmospheric air
-  protection and rational use of water resources
-  protection and rational use of lands

## Protection of atmospheric air

The main sources of atmospheric air pollution and harmful physical impact at the production sites include mechanical workshops, repair and construction areas, welding shops, forge shop, coal stockyard, outdoor parking facilities, workshops, garages, tank storage facilities, oil recovery sites, motor  transport sites, metal-working  sites, carpenter  shops,  turning  shops,  and  battery  rooms.  Dust  trapping units  such  as  centrifugal  dust  collectors  and  dust  precipitation

-  preservation of biodiversity and land care
-  technical measures.

chambers are used to remove the wood chip dust produced by the woodwork machines from air. According to the analytical monitoring schedule, the Company conducted the laboratory studies of industrial atmospheric discharges on a regular basis. The results of the above studies  showed  that  no  maximum  allowable  emission  rates  were exceeded.

<!-- image -->

The Company's	 production activities is based on the principles of of	environmental	impact,	and	saving	and	rational	use	of	natural	resources.

Deputy General Director, Chief Engineer K.G. Filippov

The  toxicity  levels  and  exhaust  smoke  capacity  of  exhaust  gases coming from engines of motor vehicles were made during maintenance in compliance with the plans and schedules.

In  2022,  the  Company  conducted  field  surveys  and  measurements of  atmospheric  contamination  and  physical  impact  on  atmospheric air, including noise and electromagnetic field strength at the borders of  the  sanitary  protection  zones  of  the  facilities  operated  by  the Company's  branches.  The  laboratory  tests  showed  that  no  healthbased exposure limits were exceeded.

58 projects of sanitary protection zones for Mordovenergo, Penzaenergo and Samara DG, branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC - were developed in 2022. The Company calculated indicators of the level of chemical and physical effect on atmospheric air outside the boundary of  24  facilities  of  Saratov  DG,  Ulyanovsk  DG  and  Chuvashenergo, branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

## Protection and rational use of water resources

The Company's facilities are supplied with groundwater from artesian wells according to the subsoil licenses and licenses for centralized water supply and disposal systems under agreements concluded with wastewater disposal organizations.

## In  2022,  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  took  the  following  measures to prevent pollution of water supply sources:

-  on-site  monitoring  of  ground  waters  at  water  intake  structures was held and there were received data on water-bearing levels, amount of ground water intake, exogenetic processes, technical condition of water wells, condition of ground water protective area and ground water pollution sources

The  laboratory  and  instrumental  studies  of  atmospheric  air  were conducted at the sources of pollutant emissions and at the sites used to stockpile waste mercury-vapor lamps and oils. All studies showed that no maximum allowable emission rates were exceeded.

At the stage of designing of the overhead transmission lines (OHLs), substations  and  other  power  facilities,  design  documents  were reviewed  for  compliance  with  environmental  requirements  with regard  to  environmental  protection,  as  defined  in  sections  'List  of environmental  protection  activities'  and  'Environmental  protection activities'.

In  2022,  the  total  emissions  of  pollutants  into  the  atmospheric  air across the Company amounted to 117.059 tons that is 1.3% less than in 2021. This decrease in emissions is due to lower consumption of raw materials and reduced running hours of the equipment.

-  quality  of  the  potable  water  was  checked.  Microbiological  and chemical parameters of water samples match the sanitary requirements
-  records  on  measured  water  consumption  and  disposal  were introduced and maintained
-  meters to control potable water consumption were installed
-  in spring and autumn, water was pumped out from the oil collectors, and the condition of the oil drain devices at substations operated by the subdivisions of the branches was checked
-  ground water monitoring devices were purchased
-  equipment at the oil storage facilities was checked and spills of oil products were cleaned up (as needed).

In 2022, the branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC took measures to fulfill the requirements set forth in the mining licenses.

In 2022, water consumption and disposal volume increased by 7% as compared to 2021 as a result of return of some employees working remotely to the offices.

## Protection and rational land use

Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  provided  uniform  conditions  for  production and  consumption  waste  accumulation  at  all  industrial  sites  of  its branches. In 2022, the Company set up 47 waste accumulation sites.

The waste  accumulation conditions meet  the requirements of SanPiN 2.1.3684-21 'Sanitary and epidemiological requirements to maintenance of areas of cities and rural settlement, to water bodies, potable  water  and  potable  water  supply,  atmospheric  air,  soils, residential  premises,  operation  of  production  and  public  spaces, arrangement and implementation of sanitary and epidemic prevention (preventive)  activities',  and  the  sites  have  convenient  access  ways for maneuvering and handling operations which is confirmed by the results of laboratory analysis of the soil at the waste accumulation sites. Based on the study findings, no pollutant concentration limits were exceeded.

The Company has license No. (52)-645609-Т/П dated January 26, 2021 for  collection,  transportation,  processing,  recycling,  neutralization and disposal of Class I-IV hazardous wastes. The work type covered by the license: transportation of Class I-IV hazardous wastes.

Pursuant  to  the  requirements  of  the  environment  protection  and waste management laws of the Russian Federation, Rosseti Volga, PJSC materialised the following environmental initiatives:

-  regular transfer of production and consumption waste which shall not be landfilled to licensed organizations for further neutralization, treatment and recycling
-  removal of felling residues after clearing rights-of-way from trees and shrubs on as-needed basis
-  repair of solid foundations at the sites used to accumulate wastes and  store  oil-filled  equipment  that  were  temporarily  put  out  of operation, on a timely basis
-  repair of the available containers and adding covers
-  installation of waste containers and purchase of trays for preventing soil pollution and oil product spill trays.

In 2022, based on the results of the production activities, Rosseti Volga, PJSC produced 9,512.379 tons of production and consumption wastes that is 10% less than in 2021. This was due to reduced stockpiling of waste mercury-vapor lamps as they were replaced with LED lamps under the Energy Savings Program.

The  volume  of  wastes  transferred  for  recycling  and  neutralization amounted to 4,483.0 t that is 12% less than in 2021. This is due to decrease in waste generation resulted from the reduction of the scope of repair, construction and reconstruction of the electric grid facilities.

## Preservation of biodiversity and land care

<!-- image -->

## Aiming at preserving biodiversity  and  land  resources  in  the branches,  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  implemented  the  following initiatives:

-    replacement  of  uninsulated  wires  with  self-supporting  insulated wires  with  the  total  length  of  1,868  km  as  part  of  the  repair and  maintenance  of  the overhead  transmission  lines (OTLs) (self-supporting  insulated  wires  allow  to  mitigate  the  harmful environmental impact of the business operations due to reduced width of corridors cut through the forest before routing OTLs and during operation of OTLs, and to prevent bird mortalities resulting from OTLs);
-  installation  of  23,946  bird  guards  as  part  of  the  adopted  bird protection programs for 2022 aiming at preventing bird mortalities resulting  from  OTLs.  The  bird  guards  were  installed  at  OTLs running through the Mordovian State Nature Reserve named after P. G. Smidovich (9 guards), Khvalynsky National Park (39 guards), Specially  Protected  Area  Federal  State  Budgetary  Institution Zhigulevsky State Nature Reserve and Federal State Budgetary Institution National Park Samarskaya Luka (235 guards);
-    according to the measures developed and approved by the Hunting and Fishing Department of the Samara region in order to protect fauna  items  classified  as  hunting  resources  and  their  habitat while clearing rights-of-way from trees and shrubs, the Company

conducted the environmental surveys and evaluated the extent of fauna  damage  resulted  from  clearing  of  the  rights-of-way.  This evaluation showed that none fauna item was damaged.

In 2022, the branches of Rosseti Volga, PJSC conducted the corporate cleaning days in order to prevent pollution of the land resources and maintain the sanitary condition.

## The employees took part in the following eco-events aimed at landscaping and reforesting of the Samara region:

-  international  event  called  'Garden  of  Memory'  initiated  by  AllRussian Social Movement 'Volunteers of the Victory' and Fund of Memory of Commanders of the Victory under the auspice of the Ministry  of  Natural  Resources  of  Russia  and  Federal  Forestry Agency under the 'Ecology' national project, as well as All-Russian Society  of  Nature  Preservation  jointly  with  Krasnoyarsk  forestry department and other organizations (the participants planted young lilacs in the form of a star geoglyph in the central park of the Krasny Yar settlement)
-  the participants of the Garden of Memory event planted 500 pine trees and 500 birch trees over the area of 5 ha
-  20 young linden trees were planted in the Alley of Power Sector Employees to celebrate 90 th anniversary of the power grid system of the Samara region.

<!-- image -->

## The participants of the Garden of Memory event planted 500 pine trees and 500 birch trees over the area of 5 ha

The evaluation of potential accidents and effective accident management is performed at all levels of the Company and includes individual  or  combined  procedures  for  responding  to  all  potential hazards.

## Technical measures

## In 2022, the Company took the following technical measures:

-   step-wise replacement of the oil-filled equipment with the vacuum equipment  which  ensure  ecological  cleanness  of  operation,  as part of the upgrading and reconstruction campaign and investment program.  In  2022,  the  Company  replaced  87  oil  switches  with vacuum switches.

To establish and implement the accident management measures, the Company develops guidelines, training schedules and programs.

In 2022, zero accidents with negative environmental impact occurred in the structural subdivisions of the Company.

-  Five oil-filled  transformer bushings were replaced with bushings with solid insulation to eliminate the adverse impact of oil products on soil. Plus, the Company repaired the emergency oil discharge system and transformer oil receivers at 1 facility.

## INTERACTION WITH STAKEHOLDERS

GRI 102-40 GRI 102-42 GRI 102-43

Identification  of  and  interaction  with  stakeholders  is  a  key  aspect  of  implementation  of  the social responsibility of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

The  Company  is  committed  to  building  partnership,  long-term, effective,  trust-based  and  mutually  beneficial  relationships  with all  stakeholders  including  shareholders,  investors,  customers  and consumers,  top  management,  employees  and  representatives  of the trade unions, employees, suppliers and contractors, consumers, state  authorities,  population  of  the  serviced  regions,  educational institutions, public and social organizations, mass media, etc.

The relationships between Rosseti Volga, PJSC and stakeholders are based on the principle of social responsibility in business.

## QUALITY MANAGEMENT SYSTEM (QMS)

QMS is a part of the corporate governance system of the Company intended  to  ensure  high  quality  of  the  delivered  services  as required by regulations, needs and expectations of consumers, and satisfaction of all stakeholders including employees, shareholders, investors and partners of the Company.

The  Quality  Management  System  has  been  implemented  in Rosseti Volga, PJSC and certified by Certification Association Russian  Register  for  compliance  with  International  Standard

## ENVIRONMENTAL MANAGEMENT SYSTEM (EMS)

EMS is an element of the corporate governance system of the Company  having  its organizational structure,  mechanisms, procedures and resources required for managing the ecological aspects of activities by developing and achieving the environmental policy goals.

The Environmental Management System has been implemented in  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  and  certified  by  OOO  Rostekhsert  for compliance  with  International  Standard  ISO  14001:2015  and national  equivalent  standard  GOST  R  ISO  14001-2016,  since

## ENERGY MANAGEMENT SYSTEM (EnMS)

EnMS  is  an  element  of  the  corporate  governance  system  of the Company that has its organizational structure and is aimed at  fulfilling  provisions  of  the  Energy  Policy  by  implementing programs for energy saving and energy efficiency improvement. The  Energy  Management  System  has  been  implemented  in Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  and  certified  by  Certification  Association Russian  Register  for  compliance  with  International  Standard

ISO 9001, since 2012.  QMS  successfully  underwent  the surveillance  and  recertification  audits  for  compliance  with the  requirements  of  ISO  9001:2008  (in  2012-2018),  and  for compliance with the requirements of ISO 9001:2015 (in 20182022).

In  2022,  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  successfully  got  through  the surveillance audit to confirm the compliance of the Company's QMS with the requirements of ISO 9001:2015.

2019. In 2019-2021, the Environmental Management System of the Company successfully underwent the surveillance audits to confirm the compliance with the requirements of ISO 14001:2015 and GOST 14001-2016.

In  2022,  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  successfully  got  through  the recertification audit for EMS compliance with the requirements of International Standard ISO 14001:2015 and national equivalent standard GOST R ISO 14001-2016.

ISO  50001,  since  2014.  In  2015-2022,  EnMS  successfully underwent the surveillance and recertification audits for compliance with the requirements of ISO 50001.

In  2022,  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  successfully  got  through  the surveillance audit and confirmed the compliance of EnMS with the  requirements  of  ISO  50001:2018  and  national  equivalent standard GOST R ISO 50001-2012.

## OCCUPATIONAL HEALTH AND SAFETY MANAGEMENT SYSTEM (OHSMS)

OHSMS  is  a part of the corporate integrated system of Orenburgenergo, a branch of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, that allows to manage risks and improve the occupational health and safety performance.

The Occupational Health and Safety Management System was implemented  in  Orenburgenergo  and  certified  by  Certification Association Russian Register for compliance with  OHSAS 18001:2007 in 2009.

OHSMS successfully underwent the surveillance and recertification  audits  for  compliance  with  the  requirements  of OHSAS 18001:2007 (in 2009-2019), and for compliance with the requirements of ISO 45001:2018 (in 2020-2022).

In  2022,  Orenburgenergo,  a  branch  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC, successfully  got  through  the  surveillance  audit  to  confirm  the compliance of OHSMS of the branch with the requirements of ISO 45001:2018.

QMS,  EMS,  EnMS  and  OHSMS  management  systems  of  the branch meet the requirements of international standards ISO  9001,  ISO  14001,  ISO  45001,  ISO  50001,  and  form  an integrated management system (IMS).

All  management  systems  adopted  by  the  Company  comply  with  the  requirements  of  international  standards  ISO  9001,  ISO  50001, ISO 14001, and ISO 45001.

## PROCUREMENT ACTIVITY

## REGULATORY CONTROL OF PROCUREMENT ACTIVITY

The  main  provisions  of  the  Procurement  Activity  of  Rosseti  Volga, PJSC  are  specified  in  the  Procurement  Policy  of  the  Company approved  by  the  Board  of  Directors  of  the  Company  on  July  27, 2014 (Minutes No. 2) and Uniform Procurement Standard of Rosseti, PJSC (Procurement Regulations) adopted by resolution of the Board of  Directors  of  Rosseti,  PJSC  dated  December  14,  2018  (Minutes No.  334  dated  December  17,  2018),  dated  May  28,  2020  (Minutes No.  417  dated  May  29,  2020),  dated  November  23,  2020  (Minutes No.  440  dated  November 24, 2020), dated April 05, 2021 (Minutes No. 452 dated April 07, 2021), dated June 25, 2021 (Minutes No. 462 dated  June  28,  2021),  dated  February  28,  2022  (Minutes  No.  485 dated March 01, 2022), dated September 30, 2022 (Minutes No. 508 dated October 03, 2022), dated October 17, 2022 (Minutes No. 509 dated  October  20,  2022).  This  standard  is  an  internal  document  of Rosseti, PJSC that was prepared to develop the Procurement Policy of  Rosseti,  PJSC  approved  by  the  Board  of  Directors  of  Rosseti, PJSC  (Minutes  No.  149  dated  April  22,  2014)  and  that  regulates the  procedures  for  purchasing  goods,  works  and  services  (the 'products'), despite their cost, by Rosseti, PJSC and subsidiaries of Rosseti, PJSC.

In  addition,  the  procurement  activity  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC is regulated by the following regulations:

-  RF civil laws that define the legal framework for the procurement activity
-  Federal Law No. 223-FZ 'On Purchasing Goods, Work and Services by Certain Types of Legal Entities' dated July 18, 2011
-  Regulation on interaction between  structural subdivisions of Rosseti Volga, PJSC intended to prepare, approve, review and implement the Purchase Plan of Rosseti Volga, PJSC Р-РВ-18-1268.04-22 that was put into effect by Order of Rosseti Volga, PJSC No. 318 dated April 21, 2022
-  orders, instructions and decrees of Rosseti Volga, PJSC
-  Purchase Plan for 2022
-  technical regulations (standards, specifications, regulatory documents).

## PROCUREMENT ACTIVITY PRINCIPLES

## THE MAIN PRINCIPLES OF THE PROCUREMENT POLICY OF ROSSETI, PJSC AND ITS SUBSIDIARIES INCLUDE:

<!-- image -->

Procurement transparency that shall  meet  the  requirements of  the  applicable  laws  of  the  Russian  Federation,  and  the degree of such transparency shall be sufficient for enabling potential  contractors  to  make  decisions  on  participation in  the  procurement  procedures  of  Rosseti,  PJSC  and  its subsidiaries.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Equality, fairness, nondiscrimination and no unreasonable  competition  restrictions  in  relationship with  purchase  participants -  suppliers  and  contractors shall be selected through a competitive process with equal  competitive  opportunities  without  discrimination  and unreasonable competition restrictions against purchase participants,  in  compliance  with  justified  requirements  to potential  participants  of  the  procurement  procedures  and purchased goods, works and services, and with account of the life cycle, if necessary.

Transparency  and  controllability  of  the  procurement activity - planning, accounting, monitoring, control and audit of the procurement activity at all stage in Rosseti, PJSC and its subsidiaries.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Targeted  and  cost  effective  use  of  funds  allocated  to purchase goods, works and services,  and  implementation of  measures  intended  to  cut  the  customer's  costs -selection  of  technical  and  commercial  offers  upon  all  prespecified price and nonprice criteria that define the economic and other required procurement efficiency.

No restrictions of access to the procurement process as a  result  of  non-measurable requirements to purchase participants.

Professionalism  and  competence  of  employees  who participate in the procurement activity of Rosseti, PJSC and  its  subsidiaries -  the  officials  shall  be  personally responsible  for  effective  management  of  the  procurement procedures  and  for  their  decisions  on  purchases;  actions prescribed  by  the  procurement  regulatory  documents  shall be performed in a flawless manner.

Compliance  with  the  applicable  laws  that  regulate the  procurement  activity  management and  with  anticorruption  laws  including  the  Procurement  Anti-Corruption Standard.

## STRUCTURE OF PURCHASES FROM SME ENTITIES

In  order  to  meet  the  requirements  of  Federal  Law  No.  223-FZ dated July 18, 2011 'On Purchasing Goods, Work, and Services by Certain  Types  of  Legal  Entities',  and  in  pursuance  of  resolution  of the Central Procurement Commission of Rosseti, JSC (Minutes No. 34  dated  November  29,  2013),  procurement  documents  contain additional provisions requesting that procurement participants and  subcontracted  entities  submit  documents  allowing  to  classify procurement  participants (subcontracted entities) as  small  and medium-sized  business  entities.  Information  about  the  scope  of contracts concluded with small and medium-sized business entities from January 2014 is published on a monthly basis at the website www.zakupki.gov.ru.

In accordance with the report approved by Order of the RF Government No. 1352 dated December 11, 2014, volume of purchases of Rosseti Volga,  PJSC  from  small  and  medium-sized  businesses  in  2022 amounted to 4,706,351.36 thousand rubles, including VAT, or 76.70%, volume of purchases only from small and medium-sized businesses amounted to 1,233,935.06 thousand rubles, including VAT, or 20.11% (in  terms  of  total  value  of  payments  in  the  reporting  year  under agreements  with  performance  times  exceeding  one  calendar  year, including agreements concluded in previous reporting periods), and 6,997  agreements in  the  amount  of  11,090,643.67  thousand  rubles inclusive of VAT were signed with small and medium-sized businesses in 2022.

## ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICY AND SAFETY OF ECONOMIC ACTIVITIES GRI 102-16 GRI 205-2

<!-- image -->

In	 present-day	 conditions,	 the	 importance	 of	 economic	 security	 rises	 exponentially, and	the	 results	 of	 work	 in	 this	 area	 have	 a	 direct	 impact	 on	 stability	 of	 production and	financial	activities	of	companies	in	the	fuel	and	power	sector. GRI 103-2

Acting Deputy General Director for Security A.Yu. Skachkov

The Anti-Corruption Policy is a framework document of Rosseti Volga, PJSC in the area of anticorruption enforcement. This document includes a set of interrelated principles, procedures, and specific measures set forth in the official anti-corruption law interpretation act - Guidelines for  elaborating  and  implementing  measures  to  be  taken  by  entities  in  order  to  prevent  and combat the corrupt practices.

## On local regulations adopted in the reporting period to meet the requirement of the federal anti-corruption laws

Activities aimed at improvement of the Anti-Corruption Policy in Rosseti Volga, PJSC are implemented on an ongoing basis. At the stage of assessment of the efficiency of corruption prevention and control measures and with account of legislative changes in regulation of anticorruption activities of organizations, Rosseti Volga, PJSC reviewed and approved the following local regulations:

-  Contractor  due  diligence  procedures  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  (M-RV-27-2571.02-22).  Order  of  IDGC  of  Volga,  PJSC  No.  411  dated May 25, 2022.
-  Regulation on managing conflict of interest at Rosseti Volga, PJSC (P-RV-27-1905.03-21). Change notice No. 2278 dated February 02, 2022.

Due to the above measures, all anti-corruption regulatory framework of Rosseti Volga, PJSC fully meets the requirements of the Anti-Corruption Policy.

## On possible facts of corruption and other abusive practices in the Company

<!-- image -->

Rosseti Volga, PJSC has in place the system to manage registration and investigation of alleged corrupt practices reported in relation to employees. In order to create a more available and open environment as  related  to  implementing  measures  aimed  at  identifying  and preventing unscrupulous actions of employees, for any person and any entity, the Company has in place the Procedure for acceptance and consideration of reports on suspected corruption and fraud cases submitted via hot line by employees, contracting parties of Rosseti Volga,  PJSC  and  other  individuals  and  legal  entities.  According to  this  local  regulation,  the  Company  has  established  different communication channels available for use ('hotline', feedback form available via the corporate website of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, etc.).

is  sent  to  the  applicant.  If  unscrupulous  actions  of  employees  are confirmed, disciplinary measures will apply against liable persons; if such actions are classified as criminal or administrative offenses, they must be reported to law-enforcement agencies of the Russian Federation.

In 2022, 12 presumed unscrupulous actions of employees of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were reported.

All applications addressed to the General Director of Rosseti Volga, PJSC  by  individuals  and  legal  entities,  and  official  requests  made by  governmental  authorities  in  response  to  reported  presumed unscrupulous  actions  of  employees  are  examined  by  the  Security Department of Rosseti Volga, PJSC. Based on results of examination of each reported case, a reasoned opinion is issued, and a response

For all reported cases, end-to-end scrupulous inspections were held on a timely basis. No acts of corruption performed by the employees were  detected.  Meanwhile,  two  cases  of  violation  of  the  local regulations  and  applicable  RADs  by  the  employees  were  reported. The liable employees were subjected to disciplinary action.

The inspection of other applications showed that no violations of the applicable laws of the Russian Federation occurred and no significant, reputational and other risks for the Company existed. The results of inspection of the applications were reported to the management of Rosseti Volga, PJSC and submitted to the applicants.

## On measures aimed at identifying and resolving conflicts of interest.

<!-- image -->

In  order  to  limit  impacts  of  private  interests  and  personal  interest of  employees  in  the  relevant  employment  functions  and  business decisions  to  be  made,  the  Company  has  in  place  the  Regulation on  managing  conflict  of  interests  at  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  (P-RV27-1905.03-21)  and  the  Internal  Rules  on  managing  activities  for reporting  information  about  property,  revenues  and  liabilities  of chief  executive  officers  of  IDGC  of  Volga,  PJSC  and  its  branches (R-MRSK-09-1669.03-19).

In 2022, the Company took measures to verify the information specified in  the  income,  property  and  liability  certificates  of  chief  executive officers  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  and  their  immediate  relatives.  The Company verified 56 certificates, including 10 documents submitted by  the  declarants  and  46  documents  submitted  by  their  immediate relatives. The information was provided to the full extent, and no facts of conflict of interest, manifestations of corruption and other abusive practices were detected.

The  conflict  of  interest  among  the  administrative  and  managerial staff  of  the  Company  for  2021  were  declared  in  2022.  In  total, 3,259  conflict  of  interest  declarations  of  the  administrative  and managerial staff of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were verified. The verification showed a number of preconflict situations that were reviewed at the meeting of the Central commission on observance of the corporate ethics norms and solving the conflicts of interest of Rosseti, PJSC. The Commission approved measures to mitigate, eliminate and manage risks associated with potential conflict of interest in the Company. The above measures allowed to remedy the preconflict situations.

The declaring results of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were reviewed at the meeting of the Central commission on observance of the corporate ethics norms and solving the conflicts of interest of Rosseti, PJSC.

In addition, 460 conflict of interest declarations of job candidates of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were verified during the reporting period, and opinions  on  verification  results  were  prepared  on  each  candidate. Negative opinions were issued for 28 job candidates as a potential conflict of interest and other significant factors were found.

## Prevention of corruption and anti-corruption activities in interaction with the partners and contractors

Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  is  committed  to  establishing  and  maintaining business  relations  with  partners  and  contracting  parties  who  also observe the anti-corruption laws of the Russian Federation.

In  order  to  implement  the  Anti-Corruption  Policy,  Rosseti  Volga, PJSC creates the information environment accessible by partners and  contracting  parties  to  have  an  understanding  of  programs, norms of behavior, procedures and rules aimed at preventing and combating corruption. The corporate website of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (www.rossetivolga.ru)  includes  a  section  titled  'Anti-Corruption Policy' that contains the relevant internal regulations and regular updates  of  material  events  related  to  prevention  and  combating corruption.  Anti-corruption  commitments  of  potential  contractors are reflected in all procurement-related documentation and notices of procurement, including all draft contracts to be concluded with the Company.

Besides,  in  accordance  with  the  uniform  anti-corruption  standard, procurement procedures and execution of contracts in the Company are  monitored/approved  by  the  Security  Department  with  respect to  verifying  reasonableness  of  a  procurement/signing  contract, assessing  the  level  of  due  diligence  and  integrity  of  contractors, disclosure of their ownership structure (including ultimate beneficiaries),  consent  to  personal  data  processing,  checking  their track records and duration of their operations on the market of goods, works and services, involvement in corruption scandals, as well as affiliation, corrupted practices associated with holding positions in the Company, etc.

During the reporting period, 5,398 lots were approved with account of the above criteria as part of the draft quarterly corrections to the Purchase Plan for 2022 and Purchase Plan for 2023. The Company also checked and approved 6,777 analytical notes on purchases in the amount of less than 500 thousand rubles, and 2,212 final protocols on purchases in the amount exceeding 500 thousand rubles. Based on the results of processing of the above purchases, the Company considered 22,399 legal entities and individual entrepreneurs.

On participation in working groups and anti-corruption collective action initiatives aimed to prevent and combat corruption (collaboration with government agencies, civic institutions while implementing the Anti-Corruption Policy).

The  Company  takes  an  active  part  in  round-table  discussions and  meetings  held  by  the  Chamber  of  Commerce  and  Industry  of the  Saratov  region  concerning  open,  honest  and  fair  conduct  of business.

The system of interaction with the state bodies carrying out control and  monitoring  functions,  and  law  enforcement  agencies  was established, that allows to coordinate joint efforts for implementation of the state anti-corruption policy.

These activities prepared and implemented by Rosseti Volga, PJSC allow the Company to mitigate, to the fullest extent possible, the risks of corruption in its business processes that is confirmed on a yearly basis  by  Class  A1  of  the  annual  Anti-Corruption  Rating  of  Russian Businesses assigned to the Group of Companies Rosseti, PJSC.

This is evidence of special attention paid by the Company's management  to  anti-corruption  policy  issues  and  high  level  of corruption prevention efforts.

## INFORMATION DISCLOSURE

In  2022,  the  Company  continued  pursuing  the  development  of  integrated  communications within  the  united  external  and  internal  corporate  information  policy  of  Rosseti,  PJSC  and Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

## Relations with general public, governmental authorities and mass media

The Company publishes both information subject to disclosure and wide range of content on all business areas of the Company on the official website https://www.rossetivolga.ru .

In 2022, the Company maintained communication with the federal, regional and local authorities and administrations.

Participation  of  representatives  of  the  governmental  and  local authorities  in  the  Company's  events  is  an  evidence  both  of  the Company's leadership positions and its contribution to social and economic development of the serviced regions.

The  Company  continues  to  foster  the  extensive  and  productive cooperation with the regional education authorities, administrative bodies of RF Ministry for Civil Defense, Emergency Management and Natural Disaster Response and Ministry of Internal Affairs intended to prevent  electrical injuries,  raise  electric  safety  awareness among population, shape principles of personal responsibility for adherence to the rules while staying near energy facilities, and to prevent  injuries  among  unauthorized  persons  at  facilities  of  the power  grid  complex.  The  Company  also  pursues  the  preventive campaign among target groups such as fishermen, drivers of heavy trucks and special-purpose machinery, gardeners, etc.

The Company established and fosters productive cooperation with major  mass  media  that  regularly  cover  the  Company's  activities basing on materials prepared and distributed by PR subdivisions.

The main subjects of publications include innovative development, electric grid complex automation, reliable power supply, access to technological  connection  services,  implementation  of  investment and  repair  programs,  production  activity,  commissioning  of  new and  reconstructed  facilities,  preparation  for  and  operation  during the autumn and winter peak loads, emergency response trainings, corporate events, occupational safety activities, professional training of the staff, electricity theft control, prevention of electric shock  injuries,  youth-focused  activities,  environmental  activities, congress and exhibition activities, etc. During the reporting period, the  Company  was  mentioned  in  the  industry  media  and  regional media more than 4,000 times.

## MEMORANDUM OF THE MAJORITY SHAREHOLDER

## Memorandum of the majority shareholder - Rosseti, PJSC in relation to controlled grid operators

## Purpose of shareholding and plans related to controlling blocks of shares

Public  Joint  Stock  Company  Federal  Grid  Company  -  Rosseti (Rosseti, PJSC) is a public Joint Stock company in which the government has a 75.000048% participating interest.

In January 2023, Rosseti, PJSC became the parent company of Rosseti Group responsible for managing of transmission and distribution power grid complexes of the Russian Federation as a result of completion of its reorganization through a merger with Public Joint Stock Company Rossiyskie Seti (Russian Grids), Joint Stock Company Far East Energy Management Company - UNEG, Open Joint Stock Company Tomsk Transmission Grids, and Joint Stock Company Kuban Transmission Grids.

## Key objectives with respect to assets

According to the Development Strategy of Rosseti Group for the period until 2030 as approved by the Board of Directors of Rosseti Group of Companies (Minutes No. 388 dated December 26, 2019), the key objective of Rosseti, PJSC in managing these subsidiaries is to develop the power grid complex based on state-of-the-art innovative technology solutions in order to meet the needs of customers and solve the problems of socio-economic growth of the Russian Federation.

## ROSSETI, PJSC DEFINED THE FOLLOWING STRATEGIC DEVELOPMENT PRIORITIES OF THE GROUP'S COMPANIES:

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

to ensure a reliable, high-quality and affordable  power supply, and contributing to the energy security of the Russian Federation to  develop  new  lines  of  business  (non-tariff  services  and customer  services)  through  digital  transformation  in  order to  ensure  the  Company's  sustainable  adaptation  to  changes across the industry

<!-- image -->

to  ensure further increase in the efficiency of the Company's core  business,  including  through  the  introduction  of  digital technology solutions and innovations to implement the principles of sustainable development.

<!-- image -->

Rosseti, PJSC owns a controlling stake in the following 14 key distribution grid companies 3 : Rosseti Kuban, PJSC; Rosseti Lenenergo, PJSC; Rosseti Moscow Region, PJSC; Rosseti Volga, PJSC; Rosseti North-West, PJSC; Rosseti North Caucasus, PJSC; Rosseti Siberia, PJSC;  IDGC  of  Urals,  JSC;  Rosseti  Center,  PJSC;  Rosseti  Center and Volga Region, PJSC; Rosseti South, PJSC; TDC, PJSC; Rosseti Tyumen, JSC; Rosseti Yantar, JSC.

Rosseti, PJSC considers these control block of shares to be strategic assets that are not subject to alienation.

## PRINCIPLES OF CORPORATE GOVERNANCE

- Rosseti, PJSC manages the Group's companies in accordance with the uniform corporate governance standards  intended  to  ensure  the  efficiency  of  business  processes,  control  of  their  quality,  and minimization of all types of corporate risks.
- As the majority shareholder, Rosseti, PJSC is fully aware of the importance of improving corporate governance  across  the  Group's  companies  and  strives  to  ensure  openness  and  transparency  of their activities, and to introduce the recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code into their business practices.
- In line with the best corporate governance practices, Rosseti, PJSC annually ensures that the number of independent directors in the Boards of Directors of Rosseti Group's listed companies is sufficient to comply with listing rules and corporate governance principles. To ensure that activities of the Boards of Directors of Rosseti Group's companies are based on principles of independence and objectivity, it is planned to maintain the number of independent directors at least at the current level.
- Representatives of Rosseti, PJSC also participate in the annual general meetings of shareholders of the Group's companies in order to establish communication with minority shareholders on the Company's growth prospects.
- Rosseti, PJSC guarantees that the Group's companies comply with market principles in their financial and business activities.
- As the majority shareholder, Rosseti, PJSC supports the initiatives of the Group's companies related to the protection of the rights and interests of minority shareholders.
- The dividend policy of Rosseti Group of companies is based on strict observance of shareholders' rights, as provided by laws of the Russian Federation, and is aimed at improvement of investment appeal of the Group's listed companies and increase of their market capitalization.

The amount of dividends recommended for payment is set by the Boards of Directors of Rosseti Group's companies based on financial performance, including the results based on consolidated financial statements under IFRS.

## CORPORATE GOVERNANCE OVERVIEW

Corporate  governance  forms  the  basis  for  defining  the  Company's  objectives  and  means  to achieve  these  objectives,  as  well  mechanisms  used  to  manage  the  Company's  activities balancing the many interests of shareholders and stakeholders.

The corporate governance model of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is a system of interactions between shareholders, the Board of Directors, executive bodies,  management  and  other  stakeholders,  based  on  distinct separation  of  powers  and  distribution  of  responsibility  between management  bodies,  assessment  of performance of functions and  tasks  vested  on  them,  efficient  risk  management  and  internal control  mechanisms,  prevention  of  corporate  conflicts,  combating corruption and corporate fraud, compliance with fundamental human rights  principles  and  ethical  standards  in  order  to  make  strategic decisions,  ensure  high  operational  and  financial  performance,  and increase investment attractiveness and sustainable development of the Company.

The efficient setup of corporate processes is a prerequisite for prompt and effective decisions taken by the management bodies in response to internal processes and external challenges.

Corporate  governance  principles  and  processes  are  set  out  in  the Company's Articles of Association and internal regulatory documents. The Corporate Governance Code of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is a document that codifies the corporate practices.

Creation of a transparent and efficient corporate governance system is one of the key priorities in the development and improvement of operations of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

Information on the Company's activities is publicly available and disclosed on a regular basis on the corporate website of the Company: http://www.rossetivolga.ru

In  accordance  with  laws  and  regulations  of  the Russian Federation, the Company also discloses information on the website of the information agency accredited by the Bank of Russia: http://www.e-disclosure.ru/portal/company.aspx?id=12131

<!-- image -->

Internal documents establishing specific procedures, principles and practice of corporate conduct are available on the corporate website of the Company:

http://www.rossetivolga.ru/ru/aktsioneram\_i\_ investoram/raskritie\_informatsii\_obcshest-vom\_i\_otchetnaya\_informatsiya/korporativnie\_ dokumenti/vnutrennie\_dokumenti\_obcshestva/

<!-- image -->

## KEY PRINCIPLES AND PRIORITIES OF THE CORPORATE GOVERNANCE OF ROSSETI VOLGA, PJSC:

- fair and equal treatment of all shareholders of  the  Company,  observance  of  their  rights and legitimate interests
- professionalism and  responsibility of the Company's  Board  of Directors and their accountability to the General Meeting  of Shareholders of the Company
- control and accountability of the Company's executive  bodies  to  the  Board  of  Directors and the General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company
- compliance with the standards of corporate culture and business ethics
- balance and efficiency of the internal control and risk management systems
- information  transparency  and  openness  of the Company
- social responsibility
- increased  focus  on  implementing  sustainability standards and principles and ESG approaches
- continuous improvement of corporate governance practices.

## ASSESSMENT OF THE CORPORATE GOVERNANCE LEVEL

## Independent assessment of the corporate governance

Since  2009,  the  Company  carries  out  independent  assessment  of the corporate governance on a regular basis. Over the last 13 years, the  quality  of  the  corporate  governance  has  been  monitored  by  an independent consultant - the Russian Institute of Directors (RID), a non-commercial partnership.

On  September  01,  2021,  according  to  the  findings  of  the  survey conducted  by  the  Russian  Institute of Directors (RID),  a  noncommercial partnership, Rosseti Volga, PJSC once again confirmed its  NRCG  7+  rating  'Developed  Practice  of  Corporate  Governance' according to the national corporate governance rating scale (NCGR®) (remained valid until August 31, 2022).

For the first time, the national corporate governance rating at the level of NRKU 7+ was assigned to the Company in 2012.

This rating confirms that Rosseti Volga, PJSC complies with Russian corporate  governance  laws  and  regulations,  follows  most  of  the recommendations set forth in the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia and individual recommendations of the international best practices in corporate governance. The Company is characterized by relatively low risks of loss of owners associated with the quality of corporate governance.

According to the assessment results, the Company's corporate governance practices continue to demonstrate positive trends, such as:

-  the scope of competence of the Board of Directors as related to approval of major transactions is expanded
-  a comprehensive control of conflicts of interest in members of the Board of Directors and executive bodies is in place
-  a regulatory and methodological framework for risk management has been developed, and an internal division responsible for key risk management functions has been established
-  the Company's website is regularly updated with a large amount of  up-to-date  information  about  the  Company and the results of its activities
-  the Company has been certified to ISO 14001 and ISO 9001.

<!-- image -->

## Internal assessment of corporate governance

In accordance with recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia and the Listing Rules of Moscow Exchange, PJSC, the Company conducts on an annual basis the internal assessment of corporate governance efficiency of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

In 2022, the Internal Audit Department of the Company assessed the effectiveness of the Company's corporate governance at corporate year-end 2021/2022 using the Procedure for Assessment of Efficiency of Corporate Governance approved by Order of Rosseti Volga, PJSC No. 684 dated August 14, 2020.

nent with the target assessment criterion applicable to a specific issue.

The results of compliance analysis of 'as-is' state of the corporate governance elements showed that, in accordance with pre-defined criteria for compliance of 'to-be' CG elements with principles and recommendations set forth in the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia, the Listing Rules of Moscow Exchange, PJSC and best corporate practices, maturity of the Company's corporate governance corresponds to the 'Developed Practice' level and is rated at the level of 85% (448 out of 525 possible scores).

Assessment  of  the  Company's  corporate  governance  status  was based on the findings of the analysis of six key elements and comprises a set of questions with answer options, each rated in scores. The  maximum score means a full compliance, and the minimum score - non-compliance of a rated corporate governance compo-

After preliminary examination of the assessment report by the Audit Committee on September 27, 2022 (Minutes No. 157 dated September 27, 2022), it was taken into consideration by the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC on September 30, 2022 (Minutes No. 7 dated October 03, 2022).

The Company continuously works to improve the efficiency of corporate governance; in particular, according to principles and recommendations set out in the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia, the Listing Rules of Moscow Exchange, PJSC and the best corporate  practices,  the  Personnel  and  Awards  Committee  of  the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC in 2022 consisted only of members of the Company's Board of Directors and was chaired by an independent director. ## Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia

In order to define whether corporate governance at Rosseti Volga, PJSC  complies with principles and recommendations  of the Corporate  Governance  Code  of  the  Bank  of  Russia,  the  Company assessed compliance with these principles. To assess the compliance with principles and recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code, the Company relies on criteria for assessing the compliance with the Code (hereinafter - 'compliance criteria') as recommended by the Central Bank of Russia in their letter No. ИН06-28/102 'On disclosing a report on adherence to principles and recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code in the annual report  of  the  Joint  Stock  company'  dated  December  27,  2021. The  compliance  criteria  allow  shareholders,  investors  and  other stakeholders to assess the Company's practical implementation of the Code's principles and the progress of their implementation, to compare the corporate governance practices implemented by the Company with practices used by other companies.

The  Company  does  not  use  any  mechanisms  and  instruments  of corporate governance other than (in lieu of) those recommended in the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia.

## GENERAL MEETING OF SHAREHOLDERS

The General Meeting of Shareholders is the supreme management body of the Company entitled to make decisions on the most critical aspects of the Company's activities. By taking part in the Meeting, shareholders exercise their right to participate in managing and controlling the Company's affairs.

On June 17, 2022, the annual General Meeting of the Company's shareholders was held, attended by shareholders holding 84.26% of the Company's shares.

According to the results of the annual General Meeting of Shareholders, the Company approved the annual report and annual accounting (financial) statements, distribution of the Company's profit  in  accordance  with  results  of  the  2021  financial  year,  including the amount of dividends payable in accordance with 2021 results, elected the Board of Directors and the Internal Audit Commission, approved the restated Regulation on the Internal Audit Commission and the Auditor of the Company.

Federation', the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC decided to hold the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company in 2022 by absentee voting.

In 2020 and 2021, the annual General Meetings of Shareholders of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were also held by absentee voting.

Pursuant to Article 3(1) of Federal Law No. 25-FZ dated February 25, 2022 'On amending the Federal Law 'On Joint Stock Companies' and suspending certain provisions of legislative acts of the Russian

At the stage of preparation, convocation and holding of the annual General Meeting of Shareholders, the Company ensured the proper functioning of the tools for remote participation of all shareholders in the meeting, including the possibility to complete the electronic voting ballots on the Registrar's website. At the stage of preparation for  the  meeting,  the  Company  maintained  a  telephone  line  and  a dedicated email address for communication with shareholders and ensured  a  forum-based  activities  on  the  meeting  agenda  on  the Company's website.

In the reporting period, no extraordinary General Meetings of Shareholders of Rosseti Volga, PJSC were held.

Results of the voting and the full texts of resolutions passed by the General Meetings of Shareholders of the Company are  available  on  the  corporate  website  of  the  Company:  http://www.rossetivolga.ru/ru/aktsioneram\_i\_investoram/ korporativnoe\_upravlenie/sobraniya\_aktsionerov/protokoli\_sobraniy\_aktsionerov/

## BOARD OF DIRECTORS 4

The Board of Directors is a collective management body of the Company accountable to  the  shareholders  and  consisting  of  equal  members  who  are  responsible  for  the Company's performance. The Board of Directors is a strategic management body mostly responsible for overall guidance and direction of the Company, including supervisory and oversight functions.

The matters reserved to the Board of Directors of the Company include such governance matters of paramount importance as definition of the Company's strategy, investment and budget planning, establishing a system of motivation and assessment of management performance, as well as supervision of the Company's assets, reliability and efficiency of risk management, internal control, internal audit and corporate governance systems.

## According to the Articles of Association of the Company, the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is composed of eleven (11) members.

The procedure for nomination of candidates to the Company's Board of Directors is established in accordance with the effective laws of the Russian Federation and Articles of Association of the Company.

A  specific  function  of  assessing  nominees  to  the  Board  of  Directors  is  vested  on  the  Personnel  and  Awards  Committee.  By  conducting independent assessment, the Committee members evaluate whether the candidates meet the independence criteria and whether they have necessary knowledge, skills, and competencies. Recommendations of the Personnel and Awards Committee are posted on the Company's website among other materials for the Meeting and are available for all stakeholders.

## Procedure for induction of new members of the Company's Board of Directors

To ensure quick induction of newly elected members of the Company's Board of Directors and the Board's Committees, an internal document 'Introduction of newly elected member(s) of the Board of  Directors  and  Board  Committees  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC'  was adopted by Order of the Company No. 282 dated April 07, 2022. This document establishes an induction procedure for effective use of their knowledge and professional expertise.

Introduction to the post starts from the familiarization of the members  of  the  Board  of  Directors  with  the  decisions  adopted  by  the General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company, including the de- cision on election of the Board of Directors of the Company in a new composition.

As part of the induction of newly elected members of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, they receive the Company's Articles of Association and internal documents regulating activities of the Company's management bodies, as well as a link to the Company's website containing internal documents that define corporate governance, risk management, and internal control systems, and other corporate documents.

- 4 Pursuant	to	requirements	of	the	laws	and	regulations	of	the	Russian	Federation	on	personal	data	protection,	information	about	members	of	the	management	and	control	bodies	of	Rosseti	Volga, PJSC	was	disclosed	upon	their	written	consent.

## Diversity within the Board of Directors

The quantitative and personal composition of the Board of Directors  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  allows  the  Board  of  Directors  to  address issues objectively and from different perspectives, to make well-balanced decisions, to timely identify and prevent conflicts of interest, and to efficiently perform other functions imposed on the Company's Board of Directors. The size of the Company's Board of Directors is in line with the industry practice and ensures that the Board  has  the  appropriate  range  of  professionalism,  knowledge, and experience. A diverse Board of Directors with different skills, background, and experiences will look at topics with a broader perspective, thus reducing the risk of 'groupthink' and ensuring balanced decision-making.

All elected members of the Company's Board of Directors have sufficient experience in strategic management and necessary competencies to make balanced and unbiased decisions in order to ensure the efficiency of the Company's operations. They are specialists in the electric power industry, strategic management, law, audit, finance, and corporate governance. Directors have experience in acting as members of the Boards of Directors or as top managers in other Joint Stock companies whose shares are included in the quotation  lists  of  organized  trading  platforms  (stock  exchanges)  and have strong positive business and personal reputation.

## Chairman of the Company's Board of Directors

The Chairman of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC plays a  pivotal  role  in  managing  activities  of  the  Company's  Board  of Directors, therefore, additional rights and responsibilities are vested on the Chairman.

Authorities of the Chairman of the Board of Directors of the Company are set out in the Regulations on the Company's Board of Directors.

The Chairman of the Board of Directors is elected by members of the Board of Directors of the Company from among the members by a majority vote of the total number of members of the Company's Board of Directors.

In  2022,  Andrey  Vladimirovich  Mayorov,  a  non-executive  director, was the Chairman of the Board of Directors.

In the Company's opinion, concurrent membership of A.V. Mayorov, Chairman  of  the  Board  of  Directors,  in  the  boards  of  directors  of

## Independent Directors

Participation  of  independent  directors  in  the  Board  of  Directors contributes  to  an  independent  and  balanced  perception  of  the Company's  activities,  critical  evaluation  of  the  progress  of  the Company's strategy implemented by executive bodies, and independent judgment in regard to effectiveness of the risk management and internal control system.

Every year, before the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company, members of the Personnel and Awards Committee of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, analyze whether candidates to the Company's Board of Directors meet the independence criteria as specified in the Listing Rules of Moscow Exchange, PJSC. Results of this analysis are presented as materials for the Company's annual General Meeting of Shareholders.

other  companies  did  not  affect  his  performance  and  effectiveness of  his  tasks  in  managing the activities of the Board of Directors of Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC.  Besides,  during  the  independent  assessment of  the  performance  of  the  Board  of  Directors  for  the  2021-2022 corporate year, the performance of Mayorov A.V., as the Chairman of the Company's Board of Directors, was highly appreciated by all members of the Board of Directors.

## Events occurred after the reporting date:

According to resolution of the Company's Board of Directors dated March 02, 2023 (Minutes No. 22 dated March 03, 2023) authorities of Mayorov A.V., the Chairman of the Company's Board of Directors, were terminated.

The Board of Directors elected A.V. Molsky to the position of Chairman of the Board of Directors.

As of December 31, 2022, the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC comprises two (2) independent directors.

Pursuant to the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia and the Listing Rules of Moscow Exchange, PJSC, on July 27, 2022 (Minutes No. 3 dated July 28, 2022), the Company's Board of Directors decided to recognize that Korotkova Maria Vyacheslavna and Kazakov Aleksandr Ivanovich,  members  of  the  Board  of  Directors, are  independent  directors  based  on  criteria  of  affiliation  with  the issuer and major shareholder (Rosseti, PJSC). In order to maintain the Company's shares in the Second Tier of the Securities List, their independence status was confirmed by the Moscow Exchange on a quarterly basis.

In exceptional cases, the Board of Directors may declare any member of the Board of Directors to be an independent director, despite such director meets the formal criteria of affiliation with the Company, a major  shareholder,  a  major  contracting  party  or  competitor  of  the Company  as  defined  in  independence  criteria  for  members  of  the Board of Directors in cases when such affiliation does not affect the ability  of  the  relevant  member  of  the  Board  of  Directors  to  make independent, unbiased and fair judgments.

Independent directors take an active part in activities  of  the  Board of Directors and are members of three out of five Committees of the Company's Board of Directors, with two Committees of the Board of Directors chaired by an independent director.

The participation and contribution of independent directors to activities of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC improve confidence of the shareholders and other stakeholders in the Company and the quality  of  management decisions, and fosters compliance with the principles of corporate governance across the Company.

## Liability insurance of members of the Board of Directors

Director's  and  Officer's  liability  insurance  is  a  type  of  insurance mandatory for the Company as set out in the Regulation on Insurance Coverage of Rosseti Volga, PJSC approved by the Company's Board of Directors on April 18, 2022 (Minutes No. 21 dated April 19, 2022).

D&amp;O  insurance  allows  to  protect  the  financial  interests  of  the Company's  shareholders,  ensure  a  source  of  compensation  for possible losses of the Company and/or the Insured persons upon occurrence of events covered by such insurance, as well as protect

the financial interests of the Insured persons by covering the cost of third-party compensation claims made against Insured persons.

## KEY ISSUES INCLUDE, BUT NOT LIMITED TO:

- On approval of the restated Internal Audit Quality Assurance and Improvement Program of the Company (Resolution of the Board of Directors dated February 17, 2022, Minutes No. 17 dated February 18, 2022).

The program defines objectives, directions, approaches and procedure for continuous (ongoing) and periodic monitoring of the quality of internal audit activities, as well as the development and implementation of the plan of actions for developing and improving the internal audit activities.

- On approval of the Innovative Development Policy of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (resolution of the Board of Directors dated May 04, 2022, Minutes No. 22 dated May 05, 2022).

Technology- and innovative development of the power grid complex is a strategic and priority area of activities of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.  Within  this  framework,  the  Company  sets  a  determined  course  towards  establishing  effective  mechanisms  to  ensure modernization of the power grid facilities, intensify scientific  and  technical  activities,  introduce  innovations,  and  improve  and develop the innovative system.

- On approval of the Energy Savings and Increased Energy Efficiency Policy of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (resolution of the Board of Directors dated May 20, 2022, Minutes No. 22 dated May 23, 2022).

The Energy Savings and Increased Energy Efficiency Policy of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is a strategic document that outlines basic principles of conserving energy resources and improving energy efficiency of Rosseti Volga, PJSC aimed at improving the efficiency of the Company's operations by adherence to the principle of transition from individual technical measures to comprehensive technology and management solutions.

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## On approval of the Road Map for the development of additional (non-tariff) services of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (resolution of the Board of Directors dated October 13, 2022, Minutes No. 9 dated October 17, 2022).

Additional (non-tariff) services are provided in accordance with the approved list of additional (non-tariff) services of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

In accordance with scenario-based conditions for generation of the Company's business plan, it is planned that achievement of target indicators will result in an annual increase in revenue. The Company's revenue from additional services is expected to increase from 670 million rubles to 3.2 billion rubles by 2026.

- Approval of the Program for modernization (renovation) of electric grid facilities of Rosseti Volga, PJSC for 2023-2027 (resolution of the Board of Directors dated December 05, 2022, Minutes No. 13 dated December 08, 2022).

The Renovation Program for 2023-2027 is developed in order to create conditions for improvement of efficiency and effectiveness of the Company's investment activities as part of technical retrofitting and renovation of electric grid facilities.

The Renovation Program for 2023-2027 is based on physical parameters and, under the existing scenario-based conditions for financing investment programs, will allow to choose the best investment areas and allocate available funds of Rosseti Volga, PJSC to ensure, first and foremost, the required level of reliability of existing power grid facilities.
