## ASSESSMENT OF PERFORMANCE OF THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS

<!-- image -->

The annual self-assessment of the performance of Company's Board of Directors and the Board's Committees is conducted in order to improve the quality and effectiveness of activities of the Board of Directors, which ultimately contributes to sustainable development of the Company in the long term.

The  assessment  procedure  is  conducted  in  accordance  with  the Methodology  approved  by  the  Company's  Board  of  Directors  on October  23,  2018  (Minutes  No.  11  dated  October  24,  2018)  This Methodology was developed in accordance with recommendations of  the  Corporate  Governance Code of the Bank of Russia and the requirements of best corporate governance practices.

Thus, in 2019-2021 self-assessment procedures for the performance of the Company's Board of Directors and the Board's Committees were conducted. Self-assessment reports were reviewed at the meetings of the Board of Directors.

In accordance with this Methodology, the performance of the Board of Directors and Committees of the Company's Board of Directors is assessed: annually in the form of a self-assessment and once every three years (if necessary) by an independent external organization (consultant) engaged on a competitive basis.

In  2022,  an  independent  assessment  of  the  performance  of  the Company's  Board  of  Directors  and  the  Board's  Committees  was conducted in the 2021-2022 corporate year. The self-assessment results were reviewed at the meeting of the Company's Board of Directors on July 05, 2022 (Minutes No. 1 dated July 06, 2022).

## Independent assessment for the 2021-2022 corporate year

Assessment of the performance of the Company's Board of Directors and the Board's Committees of Rosseti Volga, PJSC for the 20212022 corporate year was conducted by an independent consultant, VTB  Registrar,  JSC,  selected  according  to  results  of  procurement procedures.  VTB  Registrar,  JSC  has  necessary  competencies  and experience to assess the performance of the Board of Directors and the  Board's  Committees,  including  for  companies  operating  in  the power grid complex.

The independent consultant conducted the assessment by conducting a questionnaire survey among members of the Board of Directors and members of the Board's Committees, using a 5-score system, and by analyzing documents related to activities of the Board of Directors and the Board's Committees of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, including plans of  activities  and  minutes  of  meetings  of  the  Company's  Board  of Directors  and  the  Board's  Committees.  The  questionnaire  survey included  questions  allowing  to  assess  the  experience,  business reputation, conflicts of interest, and performance of each member of the Board of Directors and the relevant Committee. In addition to the questionnaire survey, the independent consultant conducted one-toone interviews (including interviews with independent directors).

## Scores of items subject to assessment:

-  the Company's Board of Directors - 4.86
-  Chairman of the Company's Board of Directors - 4.93
-  members of the Company's Board of Directors (average score for 11 members, including the Chairman of the Board of Directors) - 4.95
-  Committees of the Company's Board of Directors (average score for five Committees) - 4.97.

The  lowest  of  the  above  scores  (4.86  on  a  scale  of  1  to  5) corresponds to a mature state level.

According to independent assessment results, Rosseti Volga, PJSC complies  with  Russian  corporate  governance  laws  and  follows  a number of recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia. Activities of the Company's Board of Directors and the Board's Committees are properly organized and comply with strategic principles of the Company and a number of recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia. The structure of the  Company's  Board  of  Directors  and  its  Committees  fully  meets the  needs  of  the  Company,  its  size  and  the  level  of  complexity  of its business. Besides, the Company's corporate governance system corresponds  exactly  with  laws  of  the  Russian  Federation  and,  in terms  of  certain  characteristics,  is  in  line  with  the  best  corporate governance practices of the leading Russian companies.

In order to further maintain the effectiveness of corporate governance in the Company based on assessment results, the Board of Directors plans  to  focus  on  the  following  recommendations  given  by  the independent consultant:

-  to  increase  the  number of in-person meetings of the Company's Board  of  Directors  and  Committees,  including  meetings  using videoconferencing
-  to  consider  the  possibility  to  form  the  Audit  Committee  and  the Personnel  and  Awards  Committee  with  independent  directors' membership only
-  to consider the election of an independent director of the Company as Chairman of the Committee for Strategy
-  to analyze whether it is required to amend the Company's internal documents  that  regulate  the  activities  of  the  Board  of  Directors and  the  Board's  Committees,  with  respect  to  updating  the  key functions of the Committees required for effective operations of the Committees / Board of Directors of the Company.

## Committee for Technological Connection to Electric Grids

Resolutions  passed  by  the  Committee  shall  be  considered  as recommendations for the Board of Directors and executive bodies of the Company.

These committees are formed by and are accountable to the Board of Directors. Chairmen of the Committees submit annual activity reports to the Board of Directors, including description of business transacted and the relevant resolutions taken.

Goals,  objectives  and  competence  of  the  Committees,  procedures for  their  setup  and  rules  of  procedure  are  defined  in  the  relevant Regulations  on  the  Committees  adopted  by  the  Company's  Board of  Directors.  Regulations  on  the  Committees  are  posted  on  the Company's website:

<!-- image -->

http://www.rossetivolga.ru/ru/aktsioneram\_i\_

investoram/raskritie\_informatsii\_ obcshestvom\_i\_otchetnaya\_informatsiya/ korporativnie\_dokumenti/vnutrennie\_ dokumenti\_obcshestva/

In their activities, the Committees follow the laws and regulations of the Russian Federation, the Articles of Association of the Company, the Regulation on the Company's Board of Directors, the Regulations on  the  Committees  and  other  internal  documents  of  the  Company, including the Corporate Governance Code of the Bank of Russia.

Members of the Committees are elected from among members of the  Board  of  Directors  and  persons  nominated  by  members  of  the Board  of  Directors.  Such  persons  should  have  competencies  and experience necessary for activities of the Committee, which ensures a diverse and objective approach to issuance of recommendations for the Board of Directors and decision-making. Powers of any member of  the  Committee  may  be  terminated  by  the  Company's  Board  of Directors.

The  Committees  may  engage  external  experts  and  consultants  to perform the tasks vested on them.

## AUDIT COMMITTEE

The Audit Committee is established in order to ensure preliminary consideration of issues related to control of financial and business activities of the Company, efficiency of internal control and corporate governance system, conducting external audit and selection of the auditor,  ensuring  independence  and  objectiveness  of  the  internal audit function, functioning of the system introduced to respond to unfair acts of the Company's employees and third parties.

Corporate governance report

The Committee's activity is governed by the Regulation on the Audit Committee approved by resolution of the Board of Directors of the Company on March 10, 2016 (Minutes No. 20 dated March 11, 2016), as amended and approved by resolution of the Company's Board of Directors on October 25, 2016 (Minutes No. 14 dated October 27, 2016), on October 28, 2022 (Minutes No. 11 dated October 31, 2022).

The size of the Audit Committee of the Company's Board of Directors is defined by resolution of the Board of Directors. The Committee shall consist of at least three (3) members. The Committee members will hold office until re-election of the Committee.

In 2022, the composition of the Audit Committee of the Company's Board of Directors was as follows 6 :

| Membership of the Audit Committee acting from July 12, 2021  till July 27, 2022  Membership of the Audit Committee elected at the meeting of the Board of Directors on July 27, 2022  Chair of the Committee:  Korotkova Maria Vyacheslavna  Development Director Tekhinnovatsiya, LLC (Independent Director)  Chair of the Committee:  Korotkova Maria Vyacheslavna  Development Director Tekhinnovatsiya, LLC (Independent Director)  Morozov Andrey Vladimirovich  Legal Director of the Association of Professional Investors (Independent Director)  Shevchuk Aleksandr Viktorovich  Executive Director of the Association of Professional Investors  Kapitonov Vladislav Albertovich  Director for Corporate Finances of Rosseti, PJSC  Kazakov Aleksandr Ivanovich  (Independent Director)   |

Korotkova Maria Vyacheslavna, the independent director, acted as the Chair of the Audit Committee throughout 2022. In order to obtain an independent, objective and professional opinion on issues within the competence of the Committee, including in relation to  preparation of materials and recommendations on agenda items, since 2010 the Committee may engage an Expert to participate in its activities of the Committee. At the meeting of the Audit Committee held on August 17, 2022 (Minutes No. 155 dated August 17, 2022), Trishina Svetlana Mikhailovna, Deputy Head of the Department - Head of the Directorate for Corporate Audit and Control of Affiliates of the Internal Audit Department of Rosseti, PJSC, was elected to act as an expert of the Audit Committee.

The purpose of the Committee for Strategy is to provide assistance for  the  Company's  Board  of  Directors  in  defining  strategic  goals of  the  Company,  developing  priority  areas  and  strategies  for  its development, and assessing the efficiency of the Company's activities in the long term.

by resolution of the Company's Board of Directors on July 22, 2019 (Minutes No. 5 dated July 23, 2019).

The size of the Committee is defined by a resolution of the Company's Board of Directors. The Committee shall consist of minimum five (5) and maximum eleven (11) members. The Committee members will hold office until re-election of the Committee.

## COMMITTEE FOR RELIABILITY

To prepare and submit recommendations for the Company's Board of  Directors  in  regard  to  analysis  of  production  programs,  plans for  technical  refurbishment,  reconstruction,  new  construction  and repair of power grid facilities in terms of meeting the requirements to operational reliability and technical condition of electric networks of  the  Company,  requirements  to  operating  activities  as  related  to assessment  of  actual  results  and  assessment  of  the  Company's preparedness for autumn and winter season and other exceptional periods  (high-water,  fire  and  thunderstorm  hazard  periods),  the Company organized the Committee for Reliability.

Corporate governance report

The  Committee's  activity  is  governed  by  the  Regulation  on  the Committee  for  Reliability  of  the  Company's  Board  of  Directors approved by resolution of the Board of Directors on July 31, 2017 (Minutes No. 5 dated August 02, 2017).

The size of the Committee is defined by a resolution of the Company's Board of Directors. The Committee shall consist of maximum seven (7)  members.  Members  of  the  Committee  shall  be  elected  for  the term up to the first meeting of the newly elected Board of Directors of the Company.

In 2022, the composition of the Committee for Reliability of the Company's Board of Directors was as follows 8 :

Corporate governance report

## PERSONNEL AND AWARDS COMMITTEE

The Personnel and Awards Committee was formed to improve the efficiency of management of the Company's development, by providing recommendations  for  the  Board  of  Directors  and  the  executive bodies of the Company in resolving HR and social issues, developing principles and criteria for defining the amount of remuneration and compensation for members of the collective and executive bodies of the  Company,  and  selection  and  assessment  of  candidates  for  the Company's management bodies.

The  Committee's  activity  is  governed  by  the  Regulation  on  the Personnel  and  Awards  Committee  of  the  Company's  Board  of Directors approved by resolution of the Board of Directors on July 24, 2014 (Minutes No. 2 dated July 25, 2014).

The size of the Committee is defined by a resolution of the Company's Board of Directors. The Committee shall consist of at least three (3) members. Members of the Committee shall be elected for the term up to the first meeting of the newly elected Board of Directors.

## COMMITTEE FOR TECHNOLOGICAL CONNECTION TO ELECTRIC GRIDS

The Committee for Technological Connection to Electric Grids ensures monitoring of  the  Company's  activities  related  to  technological  connection of  customers,  elaboration  of  recommendations  concerning  internal regulations  and  standards  for  technological  connection  of  customers and ensuring operational transparency and non-discriminatory access of customers to services for technological connection to the Company's electric grids.

The Committee's activity is governed by the Regulation on the Committee for Technological Connection to Electric Grids of the Company's Board of Directors approved by resolution of the Board of Directors on February 19, 2020 (Minutes No. 27 dated February 21, 2020).

The size of the Committee is defined by a resolution of the Company's Board of Directors. The Committee shall consist of maximum five (5) members. The Committee members will hold office until re-election of the Committee.

## CORPORATE SECRETARY

The Corporate Secretary acts as a liaison between shareholders, the Board of Directors and the executive bodies of the Company.

Activities of the Corporate Secretary of Rosseti Volga, PJSC are governed by the Company's Articles of Association and Regulation on the Corporate Secretary approved by resolution of the Board of Directors dated September 30, 2016 (Minutes No. 12 dated October 03, 2016).

The Corporate Secretary of the Company is appointed and dismissed by the General Director of the Company upon a decision of the Company's Board  of  Directors.  The  election  and  early  termination  of  powers  of  the  Corporate  Secretary  fall  within  the  exclusive  competence  of  the Company's Board of Directors.

The Corporate Secretary functionally reports to the Board of Directors and administratively (due to specifics of the RF labor laws) - to the General Director of the Company.

Acting via the Corporate Secretary, members of the Board of Directors liaise with the management and forward their requests for the provision of all necessary information. The Corporate Secretary assists members of the Board of Directors in resolving both organizational and technical issues, including matters substantive to the agenda of the next meeting.

## GENERAL DIRECTOR

The General Director is the sole executive body of Rosseti Volga, PJSC responsible for managing day-to-day operations of the Company within his/her competence and for organizing the implementation of decisions made by the General Meeting of Shareholders and the Board of Directors of the Company.

The General Director is the sole executive body of Rosseti Volga, PJSC responsible  for  managing  day-to-day  operations  of  the  Company within  his/her  competence  and  for  organizing  the  implementation  of decisions made by the General Meeting of Shareholders and the Board of Directors of the Company.

The rights and responsibilities of the General Director are determined by the legislation of the Russian Federation, the Articles of Association of the Company, and the employment agreement concluded between the General Director and the Company. The terms and conditions of such agreement are defined by a person authorized by the Company's Board of Directors.

In  exercising  his/her  rights  and  duties,  the  General  Director  is  under the duty to act in reasonable good faith and in the best interests of the Company.

From December 12, 2008 till December 12, 2022, the position of the General Director of Rosseti Volga, PJSC was held by Ryabikin Vladimir Anatolievich.

Pursuant  to  resolution  of  the  Board  of  Directors  dated  December 09,  2022  (Minutes  No.  14  dated  December  09,  2022),  authorities  of Ryabikin  V.A.  were  terminated  and  on  December  13,  2022,  Filippov Konstantin Glebovich, Deputy General Director, Chief Engineer of Rosseti Volga, PJSC was appointed to the position of the acting General Director.

## MANAGEMENT BOARD

The Management Board is a collective executive body of Rosseti Volga, PJSC responsible for practical implementation of the Company's goals, development strategy, and achievement of the Company's corporate objectives.

Activities of the Management Board shall be governed by laws of the Russian Federation, the Articles of Association of the Company and the Regulation on the Management Board approved by resolution of the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company held on May 30, 2019 (Minutes No. 15/2019 dated May 31, 2015).

Pursuant  to  Federal  Law  No.  208-FZ  'On  Joint  Stock  Companies' dated  December  26,  1995  and  the  Regulation  on  the  Company's Management Board, the Company's General Director shall perform functions of the Chairman of the Company's Management Board.

According to the Articles of Association, the Management Board of the Company makes decisions on issues within the competence of supreme  corporate  bodies  -  General  Meetings  of  Shareholders  of business  entities  with  100%  participation  of  the  Company  in  their authorized capital.

The quantitative and personal composition of the Management Board is defined by the Board of Directors with account of proposal made by the General Director of the Company. The size of the Management Board is defined by resolution of the Board of Directors dated April 18, 2008 (Minutes No. 2) and consisted of nine members.

Due to the termination of authorities of Ryabikin V. A. as the General Director of the Company, by resolution of the Board of Directors of the

Company dated December 22, 2022 (Minutes No. 17 dated December 23, 2022) authorities of the Chairman of the Management Board of Rosseti Volga, PJSC are vested in Filippov K.G. until the date when the Board of Directors of the Company resolves to elect the sole executive body of the Company and determines that the Management Board of Rosseti Volga, PJSC shall consist of eight members.

In  addition  to  a  change  of  the  Chairman  of  the  Company's  Management Board,  the  following  changes  in  the  personal  composition  of  the Management  Board  took  place  during  the  reporting  period:  On August 26, 2022, the Board of Directors of the Company resolved (Minutes No. 5 dated August 29, 2022) to terminate the authorities of  Frolov  Sergey  Petrovich  (a  member  of  the  Management  Board since 2009), and to elect Kolesnikova Natalia Mikhailovna, Director for Personnel Management and Organizational Design, Head of the Personnel Management and Organizational Design Department of Rosseti Volga, PJSC, as a member of the Management Board.

## SYSTEM OF REMUNERATION FOR MANAGEMENT BODIES OF THE COMPANY

<!-- image -->

The  purpose  of  the  remuneration  system  applied  to  members  of  the  management  bodies of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  is  to  attract,  motivate  and  retain  in  the  management  bodies  those persons who have competence and qualifications necessary for efficient management of the Company. The system is geared towards providing the best balance between the dependence of remuneration on the Company's performance and personal contribution of each management body member to overall performance.

The Board of Directors and the Personnel and Awards Committee of the Board of Directors play a pivotal role in shaping the remuneration system for members of the management bodies of Rosseti Volga, PJSC. The Personnel and Awards Committee is responsible for preliminary consideration of issues related to formation of an effective and transparent remuneration and financial incentive system in the Company.

## Remuneration for members of the Board of Directors

Payment of remuneration to the members of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is governed by the Regulation on Remuneration and Compensation for Members of the Company's Board of Directors approved by the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company held on May 30, 2019 (Minutes No. 16/2019 dated May 31, 2019).

The Regulation on Remuneration and Compensation for Members of the Company's Board of Directors contains a full list of all types of payments to members of the Board of Directors and eligibility conditions. This ensures a truly transparent mechanism for determining the amount of remuneration paid to members of the Board of Directors.

## REMUNERATION FOR PARTICIPATION IN THE BOARD OF DIRECTORS:

<!-- image -->

In accordance with this approved Regulation, remuneration to each member of the Board of Directors of the Company is paid for the period from the date of election of a nominee to the Company's Board of Directors and till the date of termination of powers of the relevant member of the Company's Board of Directors. Payments are made on a quarterly basis.

The amount of remuneration paid to each member of the Company's Board of Directors for his/her participation in the Company's Board of Directors is calculated with due consideration of the total number of meetings of the Company's Board of Directors held during the reporting quarter and the number of meetings attended by a particular member of the Board of Directors, using the following formula:

<!-- image -->

## S(1)

n

amount of quarterly remuneration for participation in the Company's Board of Directors

## Rbase

the amount of a base annual remuneration number of meetings of the Board of Directors (regardless of the meeting format) attended by a member of the Board of Directors during the reporting quarter

m

number of meetings of the Board of Directors (regardless of the meeting format) held during the reporting quarter

The amount of base annual remuneration (Rbase) is defined in accordance with the Company's revenue calculated under the Russian Accounting Standards for the last lapsed reporting year before the election of members of the Company's Board of Directors. For Rosseti Volga, PJSC, the base component of remuneration is 2,200 thousand rubles.

## In addition to a remuneration paid on a quarterly basis for participation in the Company's Board of Directors, the Regulation provides for the following premiums:

## 30% 20% 10%

of the amount of a quarterly remuneration for participation in the Company's Board of Directors is paid to the Chairman of the Company's Board of Directors of the amount of a quarterly remuneration for participation in the Company's Board of Directors is paid to the Chairman of a special-purpose committee of the Company's Board of Directors (this premium is not paid if, during the reporting quarter, no meetings of such committee was held)

of the amount of a quarterly remuneration for participation in the Company's Board of Directors is paid for acting as a member of a special-purpose committee of the Company's Board of Directors (this premium is not paid if, during the reporting quarter, no meetings of such committee was held).

The total amount of a quarterly remuneration paid to each member of the Company's Board of Directors, including premiums, shall not exceed 1/4 of Rbase. (550 thousand rubles).

The Regulation on Remuneration and Compensation for Members of the Company's Board of Directors provides for a number of conditions under which a remuneration is not paid, i.e.:

-  if a member of the Company's Board of Directors did not participate in more than 50% of the meetings held in the reporting quarter
-  if a member of the Company's Board of Directors concurrently holds a position in the collective executive body of the Company or is the sole executive body (General Director) of the Company
-  if a member of the Company's Board of Directors is a person who is not subject to any restrictions or prohibitions imposed by the applicable federal law.

In 2022, no remuneration was accrued and paid to Ryabikin Vladimir Anatolievich who acted as the Chairman of the Management Board and the General Director of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (before December 12, 2022).

<!-- image -->

## Compensation for members of the Board of Directors

Members of the Company's Board of Directors are entitled to compensation of expenses related to their participation in meetings of the Board of Directors, a special-purpose committee of the Board of Directors, general meetings of shareholders of the Company, which are calculated in accordance with the rates for compensation of travel expenses of top managers of the Company applicable at the time of the relevant meeting. In 2022, compensation paid to members of the Board of Directors in connection with their official duty as members of the Board of Directors and the Board's Committees amounted to 110,935.00 rubles.

In the event of early termination of authorities of a member of the Board of Directors, no severance benefits, compensations and other payments will apply in Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

## Remuneration for members of the Committees of the Board of Directors

The Company's Board of Directors approved Regulations on payment of remuneration and compensation to members of the Committees of the Board of Directors of the Company in respect to all Committees of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

## These Regulations provide for remuneration for each meeting attended by a member of the Board's Committee:

For participation in a meeting of a Committee of the Board of Directors held in absentia - remuneration shall be paid in the amount equivalent to one minimum monthly wage rate for a first-category worker (hereinafter, the 'MMWR') established by  the  Sectoral  Wage  Rate  Agreement  in  the  Electric Power Industry of the Russian Federation (hereinafter,  the 'Agreement') as of the date of the meeting of a Committee of the Board of Directors, including indexation as established by the Agreement

For participation in a meeting of a Committee of the Board of Directors held in the form of joint attendance - remuneration shall  be  paid  in  an  amount  equivalent  to  double  MMWR established by the Agreement as of the date of the meeting of a Committee of the Board of Directors, including indexation as established by the Agreement.

The amount of remuneration paid to the Chairman (Deputy Chairman) of a Committee for each meeting at which he/she presided as the Chairman of the Board's Committee shall be increased by 50%.

There  is  no  debt  for  remuneration  payments  to  members  of  the Board  of  Directors  based  on  the  results  of  the  reporting  period. Remuneration payable to members of the Board of Directors based on Q4, 2022 results was paid in 2022.

Remuneration  is  paid  out  of  profits  generated  from  non-regulated types of activity.

Besides,  in  accordance  with  these  Regulations,  a  member  of  the Board's Committee is entitled for compensation of actual expenses related to performance of his/her official duties as a member of the Board's Committee.

Remuneration  and  compensation  is  paid  in  accordance  with  the budget of the Committee of the Board of Directors approved by the Company's Board of Directors, previously drawn up and reviewed at the meeting of the relevant Committee.

## Regulations on remuneration and compensation for members of the Company's Board of Directors provide for a number of conditions under which no remuneration is paid to:

-  to  the  sole  executive  body  and  the  members  of  the  collective executive body of the Company

Corporate governance report

Pursuant to resolution of the Board of Directors dated December 29, 2015 (Minutes No. 14 dated December 31, 2015) all Regulations on  remuneration  and  compensation  for  members  of  the  Board of  Directors  were  amended  to  include  the  procedure  for  paying remuneration for members of the Committees concurrently holding positions as members of the Company's Board of Directors. These persons are entitled to additional allowance at the rate of 10% for a Committee member and 20% for the Committee Chairman.

Information	is	given	in	Section	'Remuneration	for	members	of	the Board	of	Directors'.

## Remunerations to the Company's executive bodies

Members of the Management Board of Rosseti Volga, PJSC fall into the category 'Senior Management'.

The incentive system for senior managers of the Company is aimed to create certain material incentives motivating senior managers to perform at the top of their capacity in achieving strategic objectives and ensuring cost-efficient management. The procedure for determining the amount of remuneration and the payment scheme are defined in the following documents:

-  for  the  General  Director  of  the  Company:  the  Regulation  on Material  Incentives  for  the  General  Director  of  the  Company

<!-- image -->

adopted  by  resolution  of  the  Company's  Board  of  Directors dated June 30, 2011 (as amended and approved by the Board of Directors) (Minutes No. 1 dated July 01, 2011)

-  for  Senior  Managers  of  the  Company  (except  for  the  General Director of the Company): the Regulation on Material Incentives and Employment Benefits ('social package') for Senior Managers adopted  by  resolution  of  the  Company's  Board  of  Directors dated July 22, 2011 (as amended and approved by the Board of Directors) (Minutes No. 2 dated July 26, 2011).

## The incentive system for Senior Managers of the Company includes both material and non-material incentives.

## MATERIAL INCENTIVES INCLUDE:

- fixed component of the salary (monthly remuneration and salary of members of the Management Board in accordance with the terms of employment agreements)
- variable component of the salary (paid in accordance with results of KPIs achievement and depends on the level of KPIs achievement in the reporting year)
- compensations and benefits.

The Company concludes an amendment to employment agreements with employees elected to the Management Board. According to this addendum, a monthly remuneration is paid to such employees for performance of their official duties as a member of the Management Board.

<!-- image -->

The  list  of  KPIs  is  approved  annually  by  the  Company's  Board  of Directors and is drawn up with consideration of issues specific to the industry where the Company operates, priorities set forth in the Development Strategy of Rosseti Group for the period up to 2030, sustainability factors and ESG risks.

Achievement  of  each  KPI  have  an  impact  on  the  amount  of  the variable  component  depending  on  the  level  of  KPI  achievement, as well as the specific weight of each individual KPI in the amount of  bonuses paid; annual bonus payment depends on whether the relevant KPIs are achieved 11 .

The  General  Director  and  Senior  Managers  may  be  rewarded  for special  achievements. Special achievements are tasks (activities) that require specific delivery times, workmanship and commitment and are of special importance for the industry and the state.

<!-- image -->

Upon termination of the employment agreement with the General Director of the Company on grounds provided by the employment agreement, the General Director may be eligible for compensation in the amount not exceeding the threefold average monthly salary. By resolution of the General Director of the Company, Senior Managers of the Company may receive compensation in the amount not exceeding the threefold average monthly salary in case of termination of employment agreements on the grounds set forth in clauses 1, 3, 5, 8 of Article 77, Article 78 and clause 5 of Article 83 of the Labor Code of the Russian Federation.

## PREVENTION OF THE CONFLICTS OF INTERESTS

The Company is keenly focused on prevention and minimization of consequences of possible conflicts of interest in members of the Company's management bodies.

Conflicts of interest are resolved in accordance with approved Code of Corporate Ethics, Corporate Governance Code, Regulation on the Board of Directors and Regulation on the Company's Management Board.

In accordance with recommendations of the Company's Corporate Governance  Code,  it  is  recommended  for  members  of  the  Board

11	 Information	about	the	KPI	system	is	detailed	in	Section	'Key	Performance	Indicators'

of  Directors  to  abstain  from  actions  that  will  or  may  result  in  a conflict between their interests and the interests of the Company. The executive bodies are also under the duty to act with reasonable good faith and in the best interests of the Company.

All  members  of  the  management  bodies  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC complied  with  Article  82  of  the  Federal  Law  'On  Joint  Stock Companies' requiring to notify Rosseti Volga, PJSC of any signs of possible interest in the Company's transactions.

## INTERNAL CONTROL, RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL AUDIT GRI 102-30

## Internal control, risk management

1. The internal control system of the Company (hereinafter, 'ICS') is an element of the corporate governance system of Rosseti Volga, PJSC. The Company's ICS is intended to provide reasonable assurance that the goals in the following areas will be achieved:
-  operational efficiency and effectiveness of the Company, and safe custody of the Company's assets
-  compliance with legal requirements applicable to the Company and bylaws of the Company, including those related to the conduct of business activities and accounting record-keeping
-  provision for accuracy and timeliness of accounting (financial) statements.

Besides, the Company has the risk management system (hereinafter, 'RMS') designated to ensure sustainable continuous operations and development of the Company due to timely identification of risks (including risks of corruption) and risk management assessment in relation to risks that pose a threat to efficient business operations and reputation of the Company, health of its employees, the environment, and property interests of shareholders and investors.

The Company's RMS is based on generally accepted risk management concepts and practices.

The Company's preferred risk (risk appetite) for 2022, i.e. the maximum acceptable level of risk that the Company considers acceptable in attaining the Company's goals and is willing to maintain, was approved by resolution of the Company's Board of Directors dated May 30, 2022 (Minutes No. 25)

Target indicators of the Company's preferred risk (risk appetite) for 2022 are shown in the table below:

| - No. Target indicator of preferred risk (risk appetite)  1. Rosseti Volga, PJSC ensures reliable and high-quality power supply.  2. Rosseti Volga, PJSC ensures performance of obligations of the Occupational Safety and Health Policy and Vision Zero principles.  3. Rosseti Volga, PJSC ensures development and improved availability of power grid infrastructure.  4. Rosseti Volga, PJSC strives to reduce transmission and distribution losses.  5. Rosseti Volga, PJSC strives to maintain a stable financial position.  6. Rosseti Volga, PJSC strives to reduce (minimize) the negative impact on the environment.  7. Rosseti Volga, PJSC ensures that the rights of shareholders are respected.  8. Rosseti Volga, PJSC has a zero tolerance approach to violation of requirements established by laws of the Russian Federation.  9. Rosseti Volga, PJSC strives to ensure technology- and innovation-driven development by introducing science and technology solutions.  10. Rosseti Volga, PJSC ensures human resource development for the power grid complex and maintains a consistently high level of availability of operational personnel.   |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

Functions of ICS participants are integrated into the common management system, are in line with their roles and are embodied in the Articles of Association, the Internal Control Policy, Regulations on business units, and functional instructions of employees. The roles of the internal control process participants are delineated in line with their participation in the relevant stages of the internal control process.

## Internal audit

- 1 . The Internal Audit Department is responsible for internal audit of the Company.

The Internal Audit Department reports immediately to the Company's Board of Directors, thus enabling the Board of Directors to be engaged in monitoring and organizing activities of the internal audit subdivision, including approval of the internal audit activity plan,  progress report on fulfillment of the internal audit activity plan and cost estimate of the internal audit subdivision, approval of resolution on appointment, dismissal and fees payable to the head of the internal audit subdivision.

The  internal  audit  assists  the  Board  of  Directors  and  executive bodies of the Company in increasing the efficiency of the Company

- 2 . Results of assessment of efficiency of the internal audit process by the Audit Committee.

In  2022,  while  reviewing  materials  related  to  evaluation  of the  internal  audit  by  the  Audit  Committee  of  the  Company,  the following facts were established:

-  compliance of the internal audit activities of the Company with the Internal Audit Policy of the Company
-  compliance of the internal audit activities of the Company with the Internal Audit Definition
-  compliance with the Code of Ethics.
- taking part in training events for employees of the Rosseti Group of companies relating to organization and operation of the risk management  and  internal  control  systems,  in  the  following formats:  Knowledge  Days  (four  training  events);  conferences on topical issues and development  strategies of the risk management and internal control systems
- integration  of  the  existing  anti-corruption  compliance  system into the common internal control system and risk management system
- implementation of measures and activities of the first stage of financial stability control mechanism, support of liquidation and bankruptcy procedures of counterparties, including the creation of  a  unified  database  of  counterparties  related  to  initiated/ instituted liquidation and bankruptcy procedures.
- improving approaches to integration of risk management into key business processes
- developing  a  culture  of  risk  awareness,  conducting  training events related to organization and operation of ICS and RMS.

management and in improving its financial and business activities due to systematic and consistent approach to analysis and  assessment  of  the  risk  management,  internal  control  and corporate  governance  systems  intended  to  ensure  reasonable certainty of achieving the Company's goals.

In 2019, KPMG, JSC conducted the external independent assessment  of  performance  of  the  Company's  internal  audit. Based on this assessment, KPMG, JSC issued the auditor opinion, according to which the Company's internal audit activities 'substantially  comply'  with  requirements  of  the  International Professional Internal Audit Standards, the Code of Ethics, and the Internal  Audit  Policy.  Recommendations for the improvement of internal audit activities were also provided.

The level of satisfaction of the Audit Committee of the Company with  performance  of  the  Internal  Audit  Department  (the  ratio between weighted average of scores according to questionnaires of  members of the  Audit  Committee  voted  and  the  number  of members of the Committee voted) at year-end 2022 is rated as 'Fully compliant'.

According to the decision of the Audit Committee of the Company dated March 14, 2023  (Minutes No. 162 as of March15, 2023), the report on implementation of the working plan and results of the internal audit for 2022, including results of self-assessment of the quality of the internal audit in 2022, was agreed.

## INTERNAL AUDIT COMMISSION

The Internal Audit Commission of Rosseti Volga, PJSC is a standing internal control body of the Company responsible for conducting regular audits of the Company's financial and economic activities, its separate subdivisions, officials of management bodies and structural subdivisions of the Executive Board of the Company:

In exercising its powers, the Internal Audit Commission of the Company is governed by the effective laws, the Articles of Association of Rosseti Volga, PJSC and the Regulation on the Company's Internal Audit Commission approved by resolution of the General Meeting of Shareholders held on June 17, 2022 (Minutes No. 19/2022 dated June 17, 2022).

The Internal Audit Commission of the Company acts in the interests of shareholders of the Company and reports to the General Meeting of Shareholders. The Internal Audit Commission functions independently of the officials of the management bodies of the Company.

## KEY TASKS OF THE INTERNAL AUDIT COMMISSION ARE AS FOLLOWS:

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

Control over financial and business activities of the Company

Independent assessment of fairness of the representations in the Company's Annual Report, annual accounting statements, and the report on concluded related party transactions

The Internal Audit Commission is elected annually by the General Meeting of Shareholders. Members of the Internal Audit Commission may not at the same time be members of the Board of Directors or hold any other office in the management bodies of the Company.

The members of the Internal Audit Commission of the Company according to the Articles of Association of the Company include Five (5) persons.

In the 2022 reporting period, the composition of the Internal Audit Commission of Rosseti Volga, PJSC changed twice:

-  analysis of activities related to accounts receivable management
-  audit of compliance with the procedure of annual inventory of assets and liabilities
-  audit of compliance with laws of the Russian Federation as related to compliance with the procedure for making related-party transactions
-  other matters related to circumstances identified during the audit.

The opinion concerns the reliability of data contained in the annual report for 2021 and accounting (financial) statements of the Company for 2021 in all material respects. The internal audit revealed no misrepresentation of information disclosed in the report on related party transactions made in 2021. The above Opinion was included in the materials provided for the Company's shareholders during the preparation for the annual General Meeting of Shareholders in 2022.

## Remunerations to the Internal Audit Commission

Members  of  the  Internal  Audit  Commission  of  the  Company  are remunerated for participation in its activities; the amount of such remuneration and payment procedure are defined in the Regulation on Payment of Remuneration and Compensation for Members of the Internal  Audit  Commission  adopted  by  Resolution  of  the  General Meeting  of  Shareholders  of  the  Company  held  on  June  01,  2018 (Minutes No. 14/2018 dated June 05, 2018).

Remuneration for a member of the Internal Audit Commission of the Company is paid in accordance with his/her performance during the corporate year and depends on how actively he/she is involved in activities of the Internal Audit Commission.

The  amount  of  base  remuneration  component  depends  on  the Company's revenue calculated according to the Russian Accounting Standards (RAS) for the last financial year.

The  degree  of  involvement  is  calculated  on  the  basis  of  the number  of  calendar  days  in  the  corporate  year  during  which  a member  of  the  Internal  Audit  Commission  performed  his/her duties, and the personal involvement coefficient. The involvement coefficient is defined individually for each member of the Internal Audit  Commission  and  reflects  participation  of  a  member  in  the meetings, including the discharge of additional duties as a Chair or Secretary of the Internal Audit Commission. The actual amount of remuneration shall be calculated according to the formula set out in the Regulation.

The Regulation on remuneration and compensation  for members  of  the  Company's  Internal  Audit  Commission provides for a number of conditions under which a remuneration is not paid, specifically:

-  persons  who  are  not  eligible  to  receive  any  payments  from commercial organization, or in relation to whom any restrictions to such payments apply pursuant to applicable federal laws
-  a  member  of  the  Internal  Audit  Commission  who  did  not participate in more than half of the meetings held during his/her membership in the Internal Audit Commission.

Besides,  the  Company  shall  compensate  documented  expenses associated  with  participation  in  activities  of  the  Internal  Audit Commission  of  the  Company,  such  as  visits  to  the  Company's facilities, participation in the meetings  of the Internal Audit Commission of the Company held at the place of actual location of the Company, including performance of other tasks of the Internal Audit Commission of the Company.

The total amount of remunerations paid to members of the Internal Audit Commission in 2022 was as follows: 793,125.00 rubles.

## EXTERNAL AUDITOR

In  order  to  conduct  an  independent  audit  of  reliability  of  accounting  (financial)  statements prepared in accordance with Russian and international standards, the Company engages an external auditor.

The audit is the most efficient way to analyze the existing setup of production process and record keeping functions, maintenance of accounting and  tax  records,  correctness  of  recognition  of  all  transactions  and  primary  accounting  documents,  and  their  compliance  with  applicable statutory regulations.

By resolution of the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of Rosseti Volga, PJSC held on June 17, 2022 (Minutes No. 19/2022 dated June 17, 2022), the association of auditors (joint tenderer) consisting of TSATR - Audit Services, LLC (INN 7709383532) (a leader in the joint tender) (previous name - Ernst &amp; Young, LLC) and Audit Company DELOVOY PROFIL, JSC (a partner in the joint tender) (INN 7735073914) was appointed to act as the independent auditor of the Company.

Rosseti Volga, PJSC for 2022 prepared in accordance with RAS and the  audit  of  consolidated  financial  statements  for  the  year  ended on December 31, 2022 prepared in accordance with IFRS, shall not exceed RUB 9,812,000.02, including VAT at the rate of 20%.

During  2022,  remuneration  paid  to  TSATR  -  Audit  Services,  LLC under the agreement amounted to RUB 6,034,380.00 (including VAT in the amount of RUB 1,005,730.00).

During the reporting period, the Company's auditor (the association of auditors (joint tenderer) including TSATR - Audit Services, LLC and Audit Company DELOVOY PROFIL, JSC provided no non-audit services, and no remuneration was paid to the auditor for non-audit services.

## Assessment of efficiency of the external audit

Efficiency assessment of external audit of the accounting (financial) statements of the Company for 2022 was conducted by the Internal Audit Department of Rosseti Volga, PJSC in accordance with the 'Methodology for assessing the quality audits, auditors' reports, and efficiency of external audit of the accounting (financial) statements of Rosseti Volga, PJSC' No. М-РВ-28-2754.01-22 approved by resolution of the Audit Committee of the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC (Minutes No. 158 dated October 19, 2022) and adopted by the Company's Order No. 944 dated October 25, 2022 (hereinafter - the 'External Audit Assessment Methodology').

AS PART OF THE ASSESSMENT, AN ANALYSIS WAS CONDUCTED TO ASSESS THE QUALITY OF PROVIDED AUDIT SERVICES IN THE FOLLOWING AREAS, AS DEFINED IN THE EXTERNAL AUDIT ASSESSMENT METHODOLOGY:

<!-- image -->

- Analysis was conducted to determine the presence/absence of threats to independence and objectivity, the influence of ethical standards, values and knowledge of the external auditor in accordance with criteria defined in the External Audit Assessment Methodology, including documentary evidence of the external auditor independence in accordance with the independence criteria established by Article 8 of Federal Law No. 307-FZ 'On Auditing Activities" dated December 30, 2008.
- The assessment process gave documentary evidence of completeness and timeliness of the external auditor's performance under the audit services agreement .
- In  assessing  the  quality  of  the  external  auditor's  performance  of  the  audit  of  the  accounting  (financial)  statements of Rosseti Volga, PJSC for 2022, the results of a questionnaire survey of the management and members of the Audit Committee of the Company's Board of Directors were taken into account, such as independence, objectivity and professional skepticism, the quality of services provided, adequacy of the external auditor's resources, communication and interactions of the external auditor. 3

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

<!-- image -->

## The external auditor prepared and provided the following documents:

-  Independent auditor's opinion on the accounting (financial) statements of Rosseti Volga, PJSC for 2022 (hereinafter, the 'Auditor's Opinion')
-  Written  Information  (auditor`s  report)  submitted  for  the  Audit  Committee  and  Company`s  Management  of  Rossety  Volga, PJSC and its subsidiaries on the results of the audit of the Company's consolidated financial statements for 2022 prepared in accordance with IFRS
-  Written information (auditor report) submitted to the Management and the Audit Committee of Rosseti Volga, PJSC on the results of the audit of the Company's accounting (financial) statements for 2022 prepared in accordance with RAS.

Based on the analysis of the above documents, it was established that the auditor's opinion and written information (auditor report) provided by the external auditor were prepared in accordance with Federal Law No. 307-FZ 'On Auditing Activities' dated December 30, 2008 and the International Auditing Standards enacted in the Russian Federation by Orders of the Russian Ministry of Finance No. 2н dated January 09, 2019, No. 335н dated December 30, 2020, No. 163н dated October 27, 2021, No. 172н dated November 09, 2021.

As part of the assessment of interaction between the Audit Committee of the Company's Board of Directors and the external auditor, an analysis was conducted in accordance with the criteria defined in the External Audit Assessment Methodology, including feedback received from members of the Audit Committee of the Company's Board of Directors regarding the assessment of the external auditor's performance in terms of communication and interaction, and it was established that parameters of interaction between the external auditor and the Audit Committee of the Board of Directors comply with the requirements of International Auditing Standard (ISA) 260 - The Auditor's Communication with Those Charged with Governance.

The issue of assessing the efficiency of the external audit process of the Company's accounting (financial) statements for 2022 was considered at the meeting of the Company's Audit Committee held on May 10, 2023 (Minutes No. 164 dated May 10, 2023)  in accordance with the Regulation on the Audit Committee of the Company stating that assessment of the efficiency of the external audit process falls within the competence of the Committee.

## Events occurred after the reporting date

## DISTRIBUTION OF PROFIT AND DIVIDEND POLICY

The Dividend Policy of the Company aims to improve the Company's attraction for investors and increase the growth of its market capitalization. The Policy is based on the balance of interests of the Company and its shareholders.

Pursuant  to  resolution  of  the  annual  general  meeting  of  shareholders  of  Rosseti Volga, PJSC dated june 17, 2022, 60,833 thousand rubles were allocated to payment of dividends at the 2021 year-end in the reporting year.

The Dividend Policy of the Company is based on the Regulation on Dividend Policy approved by resolution of the Company's Board of Directors on February 02, 2018 (Minutes No. 18 dated February 05, 2018).

## COMMUNICATION WITH SHAREHOLDERS AND INVESTORS

In 2022, the Company continued to implement a package of measures aimed at communication with shareholders, investors and other stakeholders, respecting their legal rights.

The  corporate  website  of  the  Company  is  one  of  the  main  tools for  communication  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  with  stakeholders.  In addition  to  information  disclosed  via  the  Company's  website  in compliance  with  laws  of  the  Russian  Federation,  the  Company discloses,  on  a  regular  basis,  actual  and  estimated  key  financial and operational performance indicators of Rosseti Volga, PJSC. By means of interactive website tools one can learn the current share prices and capitalization of the Company.

On a quarterly basis, the Company discloses its financial statements prepared in accordance with the Russian Accounting Standards and the International Financial Reporting Standards.

The Company maintains ongoing communications via telephone and uses email box for calls and requests of shareholders, analytics, investors and other stakeholders. The Company focuses on providing consultations for shareholders on such matters as keeping personal data up to date, receiving dividends, administration of estates and other matters related to the exercise of rights to securities. Besides, shareholders may receive advice by contacting both Rosseti Volga, PJSC and VTB Registrar, JSC, the Company's registrar responsible for  maintaining  and  keeping  the  register  of  securities  of  Rosseti Volga, PJSC.

## Materiality matrix

## Influence of the topic on assessments and decision-making by stakeholders

## Definition of materiality of topics

In accordance with the GRI reporting framework, the procedure for defining  the  materiality  of  topics  that  are  material  to  internal  and external  stakeholders  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  was  conducted  as part  of  preparation  of  the  Report.  During  this  procedure,  two  main criteria were taken into account: the significance of impact of Rosseti Volga, PJSC on sustainability topics and the importance of topics for stakeholders.

Survey period: December 2022 - January 2023.

Survey format: questionnaire survey.

Number of respondents: 20.

Based on survey results, a materiality matrix was generated. Based on aggregate scores, topics are divided into three groups according to their level of materiality:

-  Group I - material topics that scored more than [2.5] points for at least one parameter
-  Group  II  -  topics  with  lesser  materiality  that  scored  [2]  to  [2,5] points for at least one parameter
-  Group III - non-material topics that scored less than [2] points for both parameters.

## APPENDIX 1. REPORT ON COMPLIANCE WITH PRINCIPLES AND RECOMMENDATIONS OF THE CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CODE OF THE BANK OF RUSSIA

| ## No. Principles of corporate governance  Criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle  ## 1 .1 The Company shall ensure fair and equitable treatment of all shareholders in exercising their rights to participate in corporate governance .  - 1.1.1 The Company provides the most favorable conditions for shareholders in terms of participation in the General Meeting, elaborating substantiated position on agenda issues of the General Meeting, coordination of their actions, as well as an opportunity to express opinion on issues under discussion.  1.  The Company provides an accessible mean of communication such as a hotline, e-mail or web forum where shareholders can express their opinion and send questions on the agenda in the course of preparation for the General Meeting. The Company arranged and provided the above means of communication to the shareholders in preparation for the General Meeting of Shareholders that took place in the reporting period.  - 1.1.2 The procedure of notifying of the General Meeting and providing materials for the General Meeting allows the shareholders to prepare for the General Meeting in a proper manner.  1.  In the reporting period, the notification of the General Meeting of Shareholders was posted (published) on the corporate website at least 30 days prior to the date of the General Meeting, unless a longer notification period is provided by the law.  2.   Notification of the General Meeting contains a list of documents required to access the meeting room.  3.  Shareholders were given access to information on persons who had proposed issues on the agenda and nominees to the Board of Directors and Internal Audit Commission of the Company (if the formation of the above commission is provided by the Articles of Association of the Company).   |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

This  Report  on  compliance  with  principles  and  recommendations of  the  Corporate  Governance  Code  was  reviewed  by  the  Board  of Directors  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  at  the  meeting  on  May  11,  2023 (Minutes No. 29 dated May 12, 2023).

The  Board  of  Directors  of  Rosseti  Volga,  PJSC  confirms  that  this Report contains full and accurate information on implementation of principles and recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code by the Company in 2022.

Rosseti, PJSC used the method recommended by the Central Bank of Russia in their letter No. ИН-06-28/102 dated December 27, 2021 'On disclosing a report on adherence to principles and recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code in the annual report of the Joint Stock  company'  to  assess  the  compliance  with  the  principles  and recommendations of the Corporate Governance Code.

| Status of compliance with the corporate governance principle  Explanations of deviations from criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle  -  observed non-observed  partially observed  -  observed  partially observed non-observed  According to Articles 2 and 3 of Federal Law No. 25-FZ dated February 25, 2022 'On amending the Federal Law 'On Joint Stock Companies' and on suspension of certain provisions of legal acts of the Russian Federation', the Board of Directors (Supervisory Board) of the Joint Stock company allowed to hold the General Meeting of Shareholders with the agenda containing issues specified in clause 2, Article 50 of Federal Law No. 208-FZ dated December 26, 1995 'On Joint Stock Companies' in the form of absentee voting in 2022.  The Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC decided to convene the annual General Meeting of Shareholders in the form of absentee voting (Minutes No. 22 dated May 04, 2022). Therefore, the notification of the General Meeting did not contain any information on the place of meeting and documents required to access the meeting room.  Convening the annual General Meeting of Shareholders of Rosseti Volga, PJSC in the form of absentee voting is a forced measure. Rosseti Volga, PJSC gives the shareholders an opportunity to prepare for the General Meeting of Shareholders in a proper manner.   |

| ## No. Principles of corporate governance  ## Criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle  - 1.1.3 Within the course of preparation and holding of the General Meeting, the shareholders shall have free and timely access to the meeting information and materials, an opportunity to ask questions to the executive bodies and members of the Board of Directors of the Company, and means to communicate with each other.  1.  In the reporting period, the shareholders were offered an opportunity  to address questions to members of the executive bodies and the  Board of Directors of the Company in preparation for and during the  annual General Meeting.  2.  Position of the Board of Directors (including special opinions recorded  in the minutes) on each agenda issue of the General Meetings in the  reporting period has been added to materials of the General Meeting.  3.  For all General Meetings in the reporting period, the Company provided the eligible shareholders with a list of entities that may participate in the General Meeting starting from the date of receipt of such list by the Company.  - 1.1.4 Execution of shareholder's right to request the convocation of the General Meeting, propose nominees to management bodies and propose issues on the agenda was not unreasonably complicated.  1.  The Company's Articles of Association set the time frame for  submission of proposals on inclusion of issues in the agenda of the  annual General Meeting by the shareholders which is at least 60 days  from the end date of the specific calendar year.  2.  In the reporting period, the Company has not rejected any proposed  agenda issues or nominees to the Company's bodies due to  typographic errors or other minor issues related to the shareholder's  proposal.  1.1.5  Each shareholder had an opportunity to use its right of vote in the  simplest and most convenient way.  1.  The Company's Articles of Association provide an opportunity to fill in  an electronic form of the voting bulleting on the website specified in  the notification of the General Meeting of Shareholders.  1.1.6  The General Meeting procedure established by the Company provides  all participants of the meeting with equal opportunities to express their  opinions and ask questions about topics of their concern.  1.  When the General Meetings of Shareholders were held in the form  of meeting (joint presence of shareholders) in the reporting period,  sufficient time was allocated to make reports and discuss issues  on the agenda, and the shareholders were given an opportunity to  express their opinion and ask questions regarding the agenda.  2.  The Company invited the nominees to the management and supervisory bodies of the Company and took measures to ensure their presence at the General Meeting of Shareholders when their nominations were brought to a vote. The shareholders could ask questions to the nominees to the management and supervisory bodies of the Company who were present at the General Meeting of Shareholders.  3.  The shareholders were given opportunity to ask questions to the sole executive body, accounting responsible, chairperson or other members of the Audit Committee under the Board of Directors at the General Meeting of Shareholders held in the reporting period.  4. In the reporting period, the Company used telecommunication means to provide the shareholders with a remote access to the General Meetings, or the Board of Directors took a reasonable decision that there was no need (possibility) to use such means in the reporting period.   |

## No. Principles of corporate governance

## Criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle

## 2 .1 The Board of Directors performs strategic management of the Company, sets basic principles and approaches to risk management and internal control system, controls executive bodies of the Company and implements other key functions.

| 2.1.1  The Board of Directors is in charge of making decisions related to  appointing and dismissing executive bodies due to negligence and other  reasons. The Board of Directors shall also make sure that the executive  bodies of the Company stick to the approved development strategy and  core areas of the Company.  1.  The Articles of Association entitle the Board of Directors to appoint  and dismiss members of the executive bodies and determine the  provisions of contracts with these members.  2. In the reporting period, the Nominations (Appointments, Personnel)  Committee considered the issue on compliance of professional  qualification, skills and experience of members of the executive  bodies with the current and future needs of the Company dictated by  the approved strategy of the Company.  3. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors reviewed the report(s)  of the sole executive body and members of the collective executive  body (if any) on implementation of the Company's strategy.  2.1.2  The Board of Directors sets long-term benchmarks of the Company's  operations, assesses and approves KPIs and core business goals of the  Company, assesses and approves strategy and business plans for core  operations of the Company.  1.  In the reporting period, the Board of Directors considered issues  regarding implementation and update of the strategy, approval of  the Company's budget, as well as criteria and results (including  interim results) of implementation of the strategy and business plan  of the Company.  2.1.3  The Board of Directors set out the principles and practices of the risk  management and internal control in the Company.  1.  The principles and approaches to arrangement of the risk  management and internal control system in the Company were  defined by the Board of Directors and captured in the internal  documents that set forth the risk management and internal control  policy.  2. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors approved (revised)  the acceptable risk level (risk appetite) of the Company or the Audit  Committee considered whether it was reasonable to refer the issue  on the corporate risk appetite review to the Board of Directors.  2.1.4  The Board of Directors establishes the Company's policies on  remuneration and/or reimbursement of expenses to members of  the Board of Directors, executive bodies and other key management  personnel of the Company.  1.  The Company developed, submitted for approval by the Board of  Directors and implemented the policy(-ies) on remuneration and  reimbursement of expenses to members of the Board of Directors,  executive bodies and other key management personnel of the  Company.  2. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company  considered issues related to the above policy(-ies).  2.1.5  The Board of Directors plays a key role in preventing, detecting and  settling internal conflicts between the Company's bodies, shareholders  and employees.  1.  The Board of Directors plays a key role in preventing, detecting and  settling internal conflicts.  2. The Company created a system to identify transactions leading  to conflict of interest and developed measures to resolve such  conflicts.  2.1.6  The Board of Directors plays a key role in ensuring transparency of  the operations, timely and full disclosure of required information,  unimpeded access to the corporate documents for the shareholders.  1.  The Company's internal documents appoint persons responsible for  implementation of the information policy.  2.1.7  The Board of Directors monitors the corporate governance practice and  exercises key functions in significant corporate events of the Company.  1.  In the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company  reviewed the results of self-assessment and/or external  assessment of the corporate governance practice adopted by the  Company.   |

## No. Principles of corporate governance

## Criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle

## 2 .2 The Board of Directors is subordinate to the shareholders of the Company.

| 2.2.1  Information on the work of the Board of Directors is disclosed and  provided to the shareholders.  1. The Company's Annual Report contains information about  the presence of each member of the Board of Directors of  the Company at the meetings of the Board of Directors and  committees.  2. The Annual Report contains key results of assessment (self-  assessment) of the quality of work of the Board of Directors of  the Company carried out in the reporting period.  2.2.2  The Chairperson of the Board of Directors is available for  communications with the shareholders of the Company.  1. The Company adopted a transparent procedure that gives to the  shareholders an opportunity to send requests to the Chairperson  of the Board of Directors (and, if applicable, senior independent  director) and to get feedback.  2 .3  The Board of Directors is an effective and professional management body of the Company which shall be able to give  impartial and independent opinions and take decisions matching the interests of the Company and its shareholders.  2.3.1  Only persons who have spotless professional and personal  reputation and knowledge, skills and experience necessary for  making decisions pertaining to the competence of the Board of  Directors and for effective execution of its functions are eligible for  the Board of Directors.  1. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors (or its  Nominations Committee) assessed nominees to the Board of  Directors in terms of their experience, skills, professional  reputation, absence of the conflict of interest, etc.  2.3.2  Members of the Board of Directors are elected through a  transparent procedure that enables the shareholders to  obtain sufficient information about nominees to have a clear  understanding of their personal and professional qualities.  1. Whenever the General Meeting of Shareholders of the Company  was held to consider the agenda containing issues on election  of the Board of Directors of the Company in the reporting  period, the Company provided the shareholders with biographic  information of all nominees to the Board of Directors of  the Company, results of assessment of compliance of the  professional qualification, experience and skills of the nominees  with the current and future needs of the Company conducted  by the Personnel and Awards Committee under the Board of  Directors of the Company, as well as information on whether the  nominee met the independence criteria as per Recommendations  102-107 of the Code and information on availability of the  nominee's written consent to be elected as a member of the  Board of Directors of the Company.  2.3.3  The Board of Directors is balanced in terms of qualification,  experience, knowledge and competencies of its members, and is  trusted by the shareholders.  1. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company  analyzed the internal needs related to professional qualification,  experience and skills, and defined competencies required for the  Board of Directors in the short and long run.   |

## No. Principles of corporate governance

## Criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle

- 2.3.4 The size of the Board of Directors is sufficient to enable its members to work in the most efficient way, including the ability to set up committees under the Board of Directors, and to enable key minority shareholders to choose a nominee for election to the Board of Directors.
1. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company considered the issue on compliance of the size of the Board of Directors of the Company with the Company's needs and interests of the Company's shareholders.

## 2 .4 The Board of Directors includes sufficient number of independent directors.

- 2.4.1 An independent director is a person who has sufficient professional skills, experience and independence to form his/her own opinion and who is able to give impartial and fair judgments irrespective of opinions of the Company's executive bodies, certain groups of shareholders or other stakeholders. In this regard, in the normal course of business, no nominee (elected Board member) shall be deemed to be independent if such nominee is connected to the Company, or is its substantial shareholder, or a substantial counterparty or competitor of the Company, or is connected to the government.
- 2.4.2 The nominees to the Board of Directors undergo the assessment of their compliance with the independence criteria, and the compliance of the independent members of the Board of Directors with the independence criteria is regularly checked. For such assessment, the content shall dominate over the form.
1.  In the reporting period, all independent members of the Board of Directors met all independence criteria set out in Recommendations 102-107 of the Code or were recognized independent by the resolution of the Board of Directors.
1.  In the reporting period, the Board of Directors (or Nominations Committee under the Board of Directors) formed an opinion about independence of each nominee to the Board of Directors and submitted a respective report to the shareholders.
2. In the reporting period, the Board of Directors (or Nominations Committee under the Board of Directors) considered the issue on independence of acting members of the Board of Directors (after election of these members) at least once.
3. The Company introduced the procedures that establish necessary actions to be taken by any member of the Board of Directors of the Company when he/she loses their independence, including the obligation to give a timely notice thereof to the Board of Directors of the Company.

## Explanations of deviations from criteria for assessing compliance with the corporate governance principle

## 1. Partially non-observed.

In the reporting period, the issue on compliance of the size of the Board of Directors of the Company with the Company's needs and interests of the Company's shareholders was not referred for consideration by the Board of Directors of the Company as the Board of Directors of the Company had no competence to consider this issue as per the Federal Law 'On Joint Stock Companies' and Articles of Association of the Company.

The Company assessed the compliance of the size of the Board of Directors with the Company's needs and interests of the shareholders in the reporting period as a part of the independent assessment of performance of the Board of Directors for the 2021/2022 corporate year. The results of assessment of performance of the Board of Directors and Committees under the Board of Directors of Rosseti Volga, PJSC for the 2021/2022 corporate year were reviewed at the meeting of the Board of Directors on July 05, 2022 (Minutes No. 1 dated July 06, 2022). Therefore, in the reporting period, the Company assessed the optimality of the size of the Board of Directors of the Company, and thus there are no risks of partial non-observance of this recommendation.

The Company will consider the possibility to adopt a practice of referral of the issue on compliance of the size of the Board of Directors of the Company with the Company's needs and interests of the Company's shareholders for consideration by the Board of Directors of the Company after necessary amendments will be made to the Federal Law 'On Joint Stock Companies'.

- 2.4.3 At least one third of elected members of the Board of Directors shall be independent directors.
- 2.4.4 Independent directors perform key functions in preventing internal conflicts and authorizing significant corporate actions.
